{"parentUuid":"a75d2b57-f897-41ed-8841-755ab21d0bc2","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"mcp_instructions_delta","addedNames":["plugin:context7:context7","plugin:serena:serena"],"addedBlocks":["## plugin:context7:context7\nUse this server to fetch current documentation whenever the user asks about a library, framework, SDK, API, CLI tool, or cloud service -- even well-known ones like React, Next.js, Prisma, Express, Tailwind, Django, or Spring Boot. This includes API syntax, configuration, version migration, library-specific debugging, setup instructions, and CLI tool usage. Use even when you think you know the answer -- your training data may not reflect recent changes. Prefer this over web search for library docs.\n\nDo not use for: refactoring, writing scripts from scratch, debugging business logic, code review, or general programming concepts.","## plugin:serena:serena\nCRITICAL: Before starting to work on a coding task, call the `initial_instructions` tool to read the 'Serena Instructions Manual'."],"removedNames":[]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"716a7cc5-b2c2-4c23-bb08-cbf1160f0229","timestamp":"2026-05-09T03:47:19.249Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"22a3281c-5649-489a-a4fd-0f2f1ab6d011","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"716a7cc5-b2c2-4c23-bb08-cbf1160f0229","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- ccs-delegation: Auto-activate CCS CLI delegation for deterministic tasks. Parses user input, auto-selects optimal profile (glm/kimi/custom) from ~/.ccs/config.json, enhances prompts with context, executes via `ccs {profile} -p \"task\"` or `ccs {profile}:continue`, and reports results. Triggers on \"use ccs [task]\" patterns, typo/test/refactor keywords. Excludes complex architecture, security-critical code, performance optimization, breaking changes.\n- attach: Port the Claude Code harness layer from this project to a new project directory. Use when bootstrapping a fresh Claude Code project on the same machine, when peers want to copy the harness, or when verifying layer separation via dry-run. Reads .claude/manifest.yaml; copies harness-tagged artifacts only; excludes stockforge biz + personal layers; generates skeleton CLAUDE.md + manifest at target.\n- crawler-reliability: Build reliable web crawlers for data gathering. Use when implementing scrapers for CafeF, NDH, VietnamBiz, YouTube (yt-dlp), Facebook fanpages, or any external data source. Covers Python/Playwright patterns, retry/backoff, selector robustness, rate limiting, monitoring.\n- ddd-tactical-patterns: Apply DDD tactical patterns (aggregate, entity, value object, repository, domain event, anti-corruption layer) correctly in Python/dataclasses code. Use when designing or implementing domain models, repositories, or cross-bounded-context communication in StockForge's 9 BCs.\n- decompose-work: Split a non-trivial task into deterministic-vs-LLM portions before execution. Use when planning multi-step work where some sub-tasks are mechanical (file ops, regex, count, schema validation) and others require judgment (synthesis, design, ambiguity resolution). Outputs structured plan that maps each portion to the cheapest competent tool. Pairs with capability-map.md (model × effort × task_class grounding) and try-n-approaches (S9 — Karpathy autoresearch loop).\n- empirical-probe-first: Probe ALL viable strategies in a multi-option ladder BEFORE committing to one. Use when a session-plan / decision-ADR / spec presents ≥3 strategy options (e.g. \"Strategy A1/A2/A3\" or \"alternative B/C/D\") and the recommended option's source-evidence may be stale. Outputs a probe matrix per strategy + commit decision with empirical citation. Pairs with `vendor-api-probe.sh` (deterministic detection layer; SessionStart hook) and `decompose-work` (deterministic-vs-LLM split per probe). Charter promotion target — codifies L-S32-1 doctrine surfaced at S32 Track A pivot (master-plan recommended A2 vnstock alternate; empirical probe rejected A1+A2; A3 SSI direct httpx adopted instead).\n- evidence-extraction: Extract structured claims from unstructured sources with citation integrity. Use when building extraction pipelines, parsing news articles or KOL transcripts, or anywhere claims are created from raw data. Ensures every claim has source_url, extracted_at, confidence, and verified attribution. Enforces no-LLM-math invariant (I-S1).\n- fastapi-module: Implement FastAPI routers following StockForge conventions. Use when creating a new HTTP endpoint, adding routes, wiring dependency injection, configuring Pydantic DTOs. Covers router boundaries, provider patterns, testing setup. Phase 2+ only — Phase 1 uses Streamlit dashboard directly.\n- grill-maximization: Doctrine for bundling Q&A questions to the human at maximum density per turn (15-20 target, max 25), so each human-in-the-loop touchpoint is information-rich. Use when composing a Q&A bundle for human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, or when deciding whether to ask one question or wait and bundle. Built on the principle that human-in-the-loop time is the scarcest resource in autonomous coding.\n- hook-diagnostics: Inspect hook event lifecycle (state machine + transcript cache + aggregator output) when triaging failed/stuck hooks or auditing self-awareness rollups. Use during sandwich-verifier review, drift investigation, or post-mortem when component-telemetry.jsonl + dispatch.jsonl + sessions-rollup.tsv need cross-checking.\n- obsidian-vault: Search, create, and manage notes in the StockForge Obsidian vault with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index maintenance. Use when adding content to the knowledge base, navigating entities/concepts, or maintaining the raw/wiki separation. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed. Extended with raw/wiki pattern from Karpathy LLM Wiki.\n- postgres-pgvector: Design Postgres schemas with pgvector and TimescaleDB for StockForge. Use when creating migrations, designing tables, writing queries. Covers vector embeddings for semantic search on Vietnamese text, time-series for price/sentiment data, JSONB vs structured columns.\n- promote-rule: Cluster agent-notes.md entries by lexical similarity, propose promotion of recurring rules to deterministic hook (priority 1), skill (priority 2), or charter (priority 3). Use when agent-notes has accumulated ≥10 new rules since last promotion run, or on-demand at phase boundary. Outputs proposal markdown to observations/. Pairs with capability-map.md (writes new task_class entries) and decompose-work (consumes promotion outcomes).\n- prompt-engineering: Design and optimize LLM prompts for StockForge pipeline stages. Use when writing or refining prompts for claim extraction, KOL recommendation extraction, sentiment analysis, thesis synthesis. Covers context assembly, prompt caching strategy, budget-aware prompts, structured output. Enforces no-LLM-math constraint.\n- qa-escalation: File-based Q&A protocol — composes bundle file in human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, tracks lifecycle (pending → answered → stale → processed), creates URGENT notification when needed. Use when intent classifier recommends OPEN_QA_BUNDLE / ESCALATE_HUMAN, or grill-maximization has a bundle ready to write. Pairs with grill-maximization (composes content) + Track 5 hooks (auto-move stale).\n- session-memory-l0-l1: Extract structured memories (L0 quick metadata + L1 deep) from a Claude Code session JSONL transcript. L0 is pure regex (file paths, commands, errors, failures incl. Vietnamese phrases, next step). L1 ships the prompt builder + response parser + 15+35 head/tail windowing — caller dispatches the LLM (no auto-invoke in Phase 0). Plus cleanText harness-stripper + TranscriptCache (mtime+size dual-check + LRU). Use when ingesting a session for memory promotion, computing what files/commands/failures the session touched, or preparing an offline LLM extraction.\n- spec-dual-layer: Write specifications with dual-layer structure (business narrative + agent contract). Use when authoring new specs, refining existing specs, or understanding the spec template. Ensures both human stakeholder needs and agent execution needs are served.\n- spec-to-wiki: Convert raw specifications to Obsidian wiki format with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index updates. Use when adding a new spec to the knowledge base, or when regenerating wiki view after spec changes. Maintains raw as source of truth, wiki as navigable view.\n- sync-pull: On-demand confidence-score lookup before a SCOPE+/ARCH/IMPL decision. Reads agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker/state.tsv + weights.yaml; surfaces the relevant category's score, tier, must-grill flag, and the decision-class threshold. Output drives \"self-decide vs grill\" choice. Use whenever an agent is about to commit a non-trivial decision and needs to ground confidence in historical hit rate per Charter Principle 8 (Calibration over confidence).\n- test-pyramid-balance: Balance unit, integration, and E2E tests properly in Python/pytest. Use when deciding what test to write, reviewing test coverage, or rebalancing skewed test suite. Covers test pyramid shape, BDD vs unit tests, when to use what. Includes stock-domain-specific test categories.\n- try-n-approaches: Karpathy autoresearch outer-loop framing — given a task or signal, generate ≥3 distinct approaches (DEEPEN / BROADEN / ABANDON families), pick one per cheap-first ordering, attach a deterministic metric function (per L-S12-1), and write the experiment frame to learning-data/loop/. Use when an open question surfaces from drift / dogfood / sync-grilling and you need to commit a measurable next step. Pairs with decompose-work (deterministic-vs-LLM split per approach) + capability-map (model × effort grounding) + promote-rule (consumes outcomes).\n- ubiquitous-language: Extract a DDD-style ubiquitous language glossary from the current conversation or domain area. Use when starting work on a new bounded context, when new domain terms emerge, or when verifying consistency between business language and code. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed.\n- user-prompt-intake: Hybrid intent classifier — main session lite-detects trivial prompts, dispatches intent-classifier subagent for complex ones. Use whenever a new user prompt lands (chat or file in human-workspace/user_prompt/) and you need to route the work. Built for full-autonomous loop where every prompt must be classified before action.\n- write-a-skill: Create new Claude Code skills with proper structure, progressive disclosure, and discoverability. Use when extracting a pattern from multiple sessions into a reusable skill, or when user asks to formalize a technique. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed.\n- ccs:continue: Continue last CCS delegation session [AUTO ENHANCE]\n- ccs: Delegate task with intelligent profile selection [AUTO ENHANCE]\n- autonomous: /autonomous — Toggle Autonomous Mode (on/off/status)\n- budget-check: /budget-check — Current Session Token Budget Status\n- devils-advocate: /devils-advocate — Adversarial Critique\n- drift-check: /drift-check — Run Drift Signals DR1-DR12\n- drill-me: /drill-me — Interactive Ubiquitous Language Extraction\n- grill-me: /grill-me — Relentless Plan/Design Interview\n- handoff-read: /handoff-read — Lightweight Last Session Load\n- master-plan: /master-plan — High-Level Work Decomposition\n- session-end: /session-end — Close Work Session\n- session-start: /session-start — Begin Work Session\n- session-verify: /session-verify — Mid-Session Alignment Check\n- spec-author: /spec-author — Create Dual-Layer Specification\n- spec-to-wiki: /spec-to-wiki — Convert Raw Spec to Obsidian Wiki Format\n- ul-audit: /ul-audit — Audit Ubiquitous Language Consistency\n- vbw-check: /vbw-check — Apply Verify-Before-Write Protocol\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch","skillCount":51,"isInitial":true},"type":"attachment","uuid":"22b37903-b0b8-4022-b34e-df6fd639d1be","timestamp":"2026-05-09T03:47:19.249Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"22a3281c-5649-489a-a4fd-0f2f1ab6d011","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

To be codified in `proposals/autonomous-protocol.md` § Drift recovery flow during S16 IMPL.\n\n### Q-2.1 — Skill-Tool Autonomous Mode Policy\n\n- **fire_when**: S5 Track 7 `autonomous-protocol.md` authoring → fired S15 PLAN 2026-04-29\n- **status**: closed\n- **fired_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 2)\n- **closed_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 2, same turn)\n- **source**: `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/2026-04-29-003-up05-askuser-permissions-rawsessions.md` cluster Q2\n- **question**: For autonomous run, how to handle Skill tool prompts (Anthropic-designed always-prompt)?\n- **options**: A: Avoid Skill calls in autonomous mode (replace with inline procedures + agent dispatch) / B: Skill calls only in SUPERVISED mode; gate autonomous loops to NOT call skills / C: Batch Skill calls at session-start under explicit user approval (one-time per session) / D: Accept friction — pause on Skill prompt; not deal-breaker / E: open answer\n- **answer**: B (Recommended) — Skill calls only in SUPERVISED mode; gate autonomous loops to NOT call skills (replace with inline procedures or subagent dispatch). Matches existing agent-notes UP-05 rule. To be codified in `proposals/autonomous-protocol.md` § Skill-tool policy in autonomous mode during S16 IMPL.\n\n---\n\n## Closed (after answer)\n\n(none yet — append entries here as questions get fired + answered)\n\n---\n\n## Implementation Note for SessionStart Hook (Track 5, S3)\n\nHook script `scripts/hooks/session-start-bootstrap.sh` (Track 5 deliverable) MUST include:\n\n```bash\n# Check queued-grill triggers\nQUEUED_GRILL_FILE=\"agent-workspace/memory/observations/queued-grill-master.md\"\nACTIVE_PHASE=$(awk '/^\\*\\*Phase\\*\\*:/ {print $2}' agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md)\nACTIVE_TRACK=$(awk '/^\\*\\*Active plan\\*\\*/' agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md)\n\n# For each ### Q-* section, parse fire_when and check match\n# If match → emit notification: \"queued-grill ready: <Q-id>; consider firing AskUserQuestion in this session\"\n# Do NOT auto-fire — surface to agent who decides timing\n```\n\nThis keeps the queue auditable + actionable without LLM-Guardian continuous polling.\n","numLines":190,"startLine":1,"totalLines":190}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f54eb722-d3cb-4137-ab0a-47652f8f4bea","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"22a3281c-5649-489a-a4fd-0f2f1ab6d011","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"ca47d52b-3785-4b67-9e88-4d3f00c048ff","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"nested_memory","path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","content":{"path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","type":"Project","content":"# agent-workspace/ — Contract\n\n> **Audience**: agents working inside `agent-workspace/`.\n> **Established**: Decision 002 § Track 1 (Workspace Dualism Foundation).\n\n## Identity\n\n`agent-workspace/` is the **agent-owned execution + memory** layer. Everything here represents **agent state, decisions, plans, observations, learning**. Agents WRITE here freely (within constitution rules); humans READ here but normally do not edit.\n\nThis separation exists to prevent two failure modes from orch:\n1. **Charter drift via shared-workspace mutation** (orch CF-DOGFOOD-2)\n2. **Human cognitive overload from dense session logs** (UP02 §1.3 — \"human đối mặt hàng chục spec/document quá tải nếu không sắp xếp khoa học\")\n\nThe agent owns this directory but must keep it AUDITABLE — every artifact provenance-linked, every decision sourced.\n\n---\n\n## Subdirectories\n\n| Path | Purpose | Lifecycle |\n|---|---|---|\n| `constitution/` | **IMMUTABLE without explicit user approval**. Charter-tier rules (autonomous-protocol, model-routing, identity-scope, etc.). Track 7 produces drafts in `proposals/`; user explicit approve to move here. | Edit denied by .claude/settings.json; Write only via human approval gate |\n| `memory/project.md` | High-level project state; phase tracker; ADRs (last 5). Update on phase boundaries + architectural decisions. | Long-lived; trim to most recent decisions |\n| `memory/current-execution.md` | **THE single routing source of truth**. Active phase + active session + autonomous_mode flag + routing table. Read FIRST every session. | Long-lived; updated each session boundary |\n| `memory/agent-notes.md` | Learned rules from real experience (post-mortem / drift / user correction). Append-only mostly. | Long-lived |\n| `memory/decisions/` | Sequential ADRs (`NNN-<slug>.md`) + `_template.md` + `README.md` index. 12-field schema (Track 2). | Append-only; supersession via status field |\n| `memory/sessions/` | One file per session: `YYYY-MM-DD-session-N.md`. What happened, decisions made, files touched. | Append-only |\n| `memory/observations/` | Subagent return artifacts (per Track 6 spec). Each subagent writes one observation file describing what it did. | Append; cleaned periodically by aggregator |\n| `memory/checkpoints/` | Session handoff state for self-reboot (Track 5). `latest.md` is canonical pointer; timestamped historical files retained. | Append + latest.md updated each checkpoint |\n| `memory/patterns-discovered/` | Pattern mining outputs (Track 0 — DONE) + ongoing pattern catalog. | Append; significant patterns promoted to constitution |\n| `memory/drift-logs/` | Drift-check (DR1-DR12) results; runs on hook + on-demand. | Time-series append |\n| `memory/post-mortems/` | After significant failure or thesis-revoked event: what failed, root cause, prevention rule. | Append |\n| `memory/thesis-log/` | Stock-domain thesis exploration entries. Read-only for IMPL sessions. | Append + revisited per calibration |\n| `memory/sync-tracker/` | Track 8a output: Confidence Score SQLite + weights.yaml + auto-rendered `_index.md`. | Live updated by hooks |\n| `memory/self-awareness/` | Track 9 output: model × effort × thinking profile cards + known-issues + best-practices. | Live updated by Stop-hook aggregator |\n| `memory/mistake-log.md` | Track 7 deliverable: structured failure catalog. What went wrong / Root cause / Prevention rule / Severity. | Append; pre-flight read by all agents |\n| `session-plans/pending/` | Plans authored, awaiting execution. Phase 0 plan lives here. | Move to `completed/` when done |\n| `session-plans/completed/` | Executed plans; archived for reference + retrospective input. | Append |\n| `quality-reports/{deterministic,probabilistic,drift-reports}/` | Tier-1/2/3 gate outputs (CLAUDE.md § Quality Gates). | Per-run files |\n| `ubiquitous-language/` | DDD glossary + domain-mapping + drift-log. Stockforge stock-domain vocabulary. | Append; canonical via `/drill-me` |\n| `calibration/` | Empirical hit-rate data per signal type. Confidence claims MUST trace here (CLAUDE.md hard rule). | Append per thesis post-mortem |\n| `research/` | Research notes from `research-scanner` subagent dispatches (e.g., reference-repo studies). | Append |\n\n---\n\n## Contract Rules (BINDING)\n\n1. **Constitution is immutable absent explicit human approval.** `Edit/Write` to `agent-workspace/constitution/**` is in `.claude/settings.json` deny list. Track 7 writes constitution drafts to `proposals/`; user explicit approve moves them.\n\n2. **`memory/decisions/` is append-mostly + supersession-status.** Don't delete decisions; mark `status: superseded-by-D-NNN` to revoke. Sequential numbering (`001`, `002`, ...) — don't reuse numbers.\n\n3. **`current-execution.md` is the routing source-of-truth.** No skill, agent, or command may hardcode phase paths or session paths from memory; resolve via this file. Stale paths in CLAUDE.md or skills = anti-pattern.\n\n4. **Never edit `obsidian-vault/raw/`.** Immutable. Vault writes only to `wiki/`. (Established in stockforge CLAUDE.md.)\n\n5. **Provenance is mandatory.** Every decision file references its source (human prompt / spec clause / drift detection / agent inference). Every thesis cites source + as-of date (I-S2). Every confidence claim cites historical hit rate (I-S20-equivalent).\n\n6. **Agents never `git commit` in this directory unless user explicitly requests.** Stage only.\n\n7. **`patterns-discovered/` artifacts are READ-ONLY for production code.** Patterns must be promoted to constitution / skills / hook scripts before becoming binding. (Otherwise they're informal notes, not contracts.)\n\n---\n\n## Reading Priority for Agent\n\nPer `agent-workspace/constitution/autonomous-protocol.md` (Track 7) and stockforge `CLAUDE.md` § Session Protocol:\n\n1. **`memory/current-execution.md`** — first; resolve active track\n2. **`memory/project.md`** — project state\n3. **`memory/checkpoints/latest.md`** if a recent checkpoint exists (within last 24h) — resume context\n4. **`memory/agent-notes.md`** — learned rules\n5. **`memory/mistake-log.md`** — pre-flight failure catalog\n6. **Last 3 files in `memory/sessions/`** — recent context\n7. **`session-plans/pending/<active-plan>.md`** — current plan\n8. **Relevant constitution files** as task demands (don't load all every session)\n9. **Relevant skills + agents + commands** as task demands\n\n---\n\n## Anti-Patterns to Avoid\n\n(From `memory/patterns-discovered/SYNTHESIS.md` § 6, full catalog 23 items. Highest-priority for this directory:)\n\n- **AP-1 Same-agent self-review** — never review your own implementation; dispatch fresh-context verifier\n- **AP-2 Self-track wind-down** — `.transcript-tokens` real-transcript is authoritative, not LLM self-track\n- **AP-5 Charter-coherence defer overriding user-CRITICAL** — re-read all `user_prompt/*` at every phase entry; never silently defer USER-CRITICAL\n- **AP-7 Performative SC ticking** — defer with explicit prerequisites > fire vacuous proposals\n- **AP-8 Pre-staged work causing checkpoint drift** — update `current-execution.md` immediately on task complete, not session-end\n- **AP-17 Identity drift** — stockforge is AI-first VN stock advisory; not generic financial framework (see `constitution/identity-scope.md` once Track 7 ships)\n- **AP-23 Continuous LLM-Guardian** — deterministic hooks = Guardian; LLM Guardian only at session-end aggregation\n\n---\n\n## Connection to human-workspace/\n\nThis directory is the agent's domain. Human's domain is `human-workspace/`. The two communicate through:\n- Human → Agent: `human-workspace/user_prompt/`, `human-workspace/decisions/`\n- Agent → Human: `human-workspace/notifications/`, `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/`\n- Bidirectional: `human-workspace/q-and-a/answered/` — agent writes pending; either human or agent (per Auto-mv rule below) moves resolved bundles to answered/\n- Audit trail: this directory's `memory/decisions/` references `human-workspace/` source files via path pointers\n\n**Auto-mv rule (HH-E.2 — D-031 ratification, 2026-05-05)**: Agent MAY mv a bundle from `q-and-a/pending/` to `q-and-a/answered/` IFF ALL of the following hold:\n\n1. **Frontmatter signal**: bundle frontmatter `status:` field value starts with one of: `answered-`, `closed-`, `resolved-`. Examples already in repo: `answered-via-chat`, `answered-via-AskUserQuestion`, `answered-2026-05-04-via-chat`. Detection is deterministic (head -20 of file + grep `^status:`).\n2. **No human-veto signal**: bundle frontmatter has NO `wait_until:` ISO-8601 timestamp greater than current epoch — if present, agent MUST defer mv until that timestamp passes. Allows human to override auto-mv per-bundle without contract amendment.\n3. **No global pause**: file `human-workspace/q-and-a/.auto-mv-paused` does NOT exist — global kill switch for the auto-mv mechanism (single empty file presence pauses all auto-mv).\n4. **Hook validation**: the mv is performed by `scripts/hooks/qa-pending-auto-mover.sh` (Stop hook). Direct manual `mv` invocation by agent (e.g. via Bash tool) is STILL forbidden — only the validated hook path is authorized.\n\nAgent never writes to `human-workspace/` outside the designated `q-and-a/pending/` write channel + the auto-mv rule above + `notifications/` write channel (existing).\n","contentDiffersFromDisk":false},"displayPath":"agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md"},"type":"attachment","uuid":"f1b6d599-7c05-4a12-bfa0-48f6c7457aaa","timestamp":"2026-05-09T03:47:26.925Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"22a3281c-5649-489a-a4fd-0f2f1ab6d011","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"ac3e8b11-a71c-4bf0-a01c-12782ed00153","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"f48b14d4-9a35-493f-816b-98ff31d56197","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WxV8BwpDHgrXyj2XyF5oNR","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t#!/usr/bin/env bash\n2\t# effort-escalation-detector.sh — recommend effort level bump based on prompt signals\n3\t# Hooks: UserPromptSubmit + PreToolUse (Agent only)\n4\t# Per routing-config.md § 4 + S65 user directive \"tận dụng full effort ladder\"\n5\t# Output: stderr \"effort_recommendation: <level> | reason: <text>\" (advisory; non-blocking)\n6\t#\n7\t# Detection ladder (deterministic regex match):\n8\t#   medium → high:    spec ambiguity / 2+ alternatives / debug stuck / cross-BC / blocker / unclear\n9\t#   high → xhigh:     novel pattern / cross-system / charter-tier touch / first-time / no precedent\n10\t#   xhigh → max:      multi-perspective adversarial 3+ viewpoints / theorem-like / recurring M-S<N>-<M>\n11\t#   any → low:        pure file-ops / mtime check / mechanical mv / pure read-and-cite\n12\t\n13\tset -uo pipefail\n14\ttrap 'exit 0' ERR\n15\t\n16\tPAYLOAD=\"$(cat 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n17\t[ -n \"$PAYLOAD\" ] || exit 0\n18\t\n19\teval \"$(printf '%s' \"$PAYLOAD\" | node -e \"\n20\tlet s=''; then\n65\t  touch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true\n66\t  exit 0\n67\tfi\n68\t\n69\tset +o pipefail\n70\t\n71\t# ============================================================================\n72\t# Step 1: pull last 5 session-block ## S<N> header lines (newest-first since\n73\t# current-execution.md prepends new sessions at top).\n74\t# ============================================================================\n75\tHEADERS=$(grep -m 5 -E '^## S[0-9]+[a-z]?[[:space:]]+—[[:space:]]+Phase' \"$CE\" 2>/dev/null)\n76\tHEADER_COUNT=$(echo \"$HEADERS\" | grep -c '^## S' 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n77\t[[ \"$HEADER_COUNT\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || HEADER_COUNT=0\n78\t\n79\tif [ \"$HEADER_COUNT\" -lt 1 ]; then\n148\t  exit 0\n149\tfi\n150\t\n151\t# ============================================================================\n152\t# Step 5: emit signal on RED/YELLOW.\n153\t# ============================================================================\n154\tif [ \"$STATE\" = \"GREEN\" ]; then\n155\t  touch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true\n156\t  exit 0\n157\tfi\n158\t\n159\t# Audit log entry capturing escape trigger (richer than .session-hooks.log).\n160\t{\n161\t  printf '[%s] %s state=%s consecutive=%d latest=%s(%s) pending_plans=%d session=%s event=%s\\n' \\\n162\t    \"$TS\" \"ESCAPE-TRIGGER\" \"$STATE\" \"$CONSECUTIVE\" \"$LATEST_SID\" \"$LATEST_LABEL\" \"$PENDING_PLANS\" \"$SID\" \"$EVENT\"\n163\t} >> \"$IDLE_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || true\n164\t\n165\tREMINDER_BODY=\"IDLE-ESCAPE-DETECTOR: state=$STATE (consecutive_idle=$CONSECUTIVE; Charter Principle 8 + AP-5 binding.\"\n172\t\n173\t# UserPromptSubmit emission via Claude Code JSON contract (mirrors\n174\t# harness-health-self-scan.sh § UserPromptSubmit emit; node may be unavailable\n175\t# in stripped environments → fallback to stderr).\n176\tif [ \"$EVENT\" = \"UserPromptSubmit\" ]; then\n177\t  if command -v node >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n178\t    node -e \"process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({hookSpecificOutput:{hookEventName:'UserPromptSubmit',additionalContext:process.argv[1]}}))\" \"$REMINDER_BODY\" 2>/dev/null || \\\n179\t      echo \"[idle-escape] $REMINDER_BODY\" >&2\n180\t  else\n181\t    echo \"[idle-escape] $REMINDER_BODY\" >&2\n182\t  fi\n183\telif [ \"$EVENT\" = \"SessionStart\" ]; then\n184\t  echo \"[idle-escape] $REMINDER_BODY\" >&2\n185\telse\n186\t  echo \"[idle-escape] event=$EVENT $REMINDER_BODY\" >&2\n187\tfi\n188\t\n189\ttouch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true\n190\texit 0\n191\t"}]},"uuid":"03f8498f-c29a-45db-b510-8ac6b4acdf04","timestamp":"2026-05-09T03:47:39.807Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\idle-escape-detector.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# idle-escape-detector.sh — UserPromptSubmit + SessionStart hook.\n# Detects ≥3 consecutive ROUTINE-IDLE / META-CADENCE session blocks at the top\n# (newest) of agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md and demands an escape\n# action (phase-audit subagent dispatch OR Q&A escalation) before agent slips\n# into a 4th idle session.\n#\n# Phase 3.5 / S175 deliverable. M-S171-1 prevention rule #3.\n# Pairs with phase-status-coherence.sh (same session; complementary check).\n#\n# Source-of-truth pattern markers (anchored from S148-S170 production blocks):\n#   ## S<N> — Phase <X> — ROUTINE-IDLE: ...\n#   ## S<N> — Phase <X> — META-CADENCE: ...\n# Both are zero-substantive-work session classes. ≥3 consecutive at top = the\n# 22-session stall pattern that surfaced as M-S171-1 (S148-S170, ~100K wasted main).\n#\n# Per CLAUDE.md hard rule + Phase 3.5 Hard Rule #2: every hook ships with companion\n# firing-test (scripts/hooks/firing-tests/idle-escape-detector-fire-test.sh).\n# Per UP-05 + L-S11-1: bash + POSIX tools only; no Skill tool calls.\n# Exit 0 ALWAYS (informational, not blocking).\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nSID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-unknown}\"\nEVENT=\"${CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT:-UserPromptSubmit}\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\nCE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\"\nPLANS_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/session-plans/pending\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nIDLE_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.idle-escape.log\"\n\n# Per-hour-bucket idempotency marker per L-S108-1 (CLAUDE_SESSION_ID may be empty\n# under SessionStart on Windows → fallback to constant collides across sessions).\nBUCKET=\"$(date +%Y%m%d-%H 2>/dev/null)\"\n[ -z \"$BUCKET\" ] && BUCKET=\"unbucketed-$$\"\nMARKER=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.idle-escape-fired-${BUCKET}\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date '+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S')\"\n\n# Per-session 5-min cache (mirrors harness-health-self-scan.sh) to avoid re-firing\n# on every UserPromptSubmit within same session — perf budget on UserPromptSubmit\n# is <2s.\nCACHE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.idle-escape-cache-${SID}\"\nif [ -f \"$CACHE\" ]; then\n  CACHE_MTIME=$(stat -c %Y \"$CACHE\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$CACHE\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n  AGE=$(( $(date +%s) - CACHE_MTIME ))\n  if [ \"$AGE\" -lt 300 ]; then\n    echo \"[$TS] idle-escape-detector: CACHE-HIT age=${AGE}s session=$SID\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || true\n    exit 0\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Cleanup stale hour-bucket markers from prior buckets (L-S108-1 prevention).\nfor old_marker in \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"/.idle-escape-fired-*; do\n  [ -f \"$old_marker\" ] || continue\n  case \"$old_marker\" in\n    *\".idle-escape-fired-${BUCKET}\") ;;\n    *) rm -f \"$old_marker\" 2>/dev/null || true ;;\n  esac\ndone\n\n# Bail gracefully if current-execution.md missing.\nif [ ! -f \"$CE\" ]; then\n  touch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  exit 0\nfi\n\nset +o pipefail\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 1: pull last 5 session-block ## S<N> header lines (newest-first since\n# current-execution.md prepends new sessions at top).\n# ============================================================================\nHEADERS=$(grep -m 5 -E '^## S[0-9]+[a-z]?[[:space:]]+—[[:space:]]+Phase' \"$CE\" 2>/dev/null)\nHEADER_COUNT=$(echo \"$HEADERS\" | grep -c '^## S' 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n[[ \"$HEADER_COUNT\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || HEADER_COUNT=0\n\nif [ \"$HEADER_COUNT\" -lt 1 ]; then\n  touch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  echo \"session=$SID ts=$TS state=NO-HEADERS detail=cannot-parse-blocks\" > \"$CACHE\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 2: count consecutive ROUTINE-IDLE / META-CADENCE among newest blocks.\n# Stop counting at first non-idle header.\n# ============================================================================\nCONSECUTIVE=0\nLATEST_LABEL=\"\"\nLATEST_SID=\"\"\nwhile IFS= read -r line; do\n  [ -z \"$line\" ] && continue\n  if [ -z \"$LATEST_LABEL\" ]; then\n    LATEST_SID=$(echo \"$line\" | sed -E 's/^## (S[0-9]+[a-z]?).*/\\1/')\n    if echo \"$line\" | grep -qE '— ROUTINE-IDLE'; then LATEST_LABEL=\"ROUTINE-IDLE\"\n    elif echo \"$line\" | grep -qE '— META-CADENCE'; then LATEST_LABEL=\"META-CADENCE\"\n    else LATEST_LABEL=\"ACTIVE\"\n    fi\n  fi\n  if echo \"$line\" | grep -qE '— (ROUTINE-IDLE|META-CADENCE)'; then\n    CONSECUTIVE=$((CONSECUTIVE + 1))\n  else\n    break\n  fi\ndone <<< \"$HEADERS\"\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 3: scan session-plans/pending/ for queued plans (informational boost —\n# idle while plans are queued = stronger escape signal).\n# ============================================================================\nPENDING_PLANS=0\nif [ -d \"$PLANS_DIR\" ]; then\n  PENDING_PLANS=$(find \"$PLANS_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -name '*.md' -type f 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\n  [[ \"$PENDING_PLANS\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || PENDING_PLANS=0\nfi\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 4: severity classification.\n#   - GREEN: 0-2 consecutive idle\n#   - YELLOW: ≥3 consecutive idle, no pending plans\n#   - RED: ≥3 consecutive idle AND pending plans ≥1 (work queued + ignored)\n# Threshold ≥3 anchors to L-S171-1 / M-S171-1 doctrine.\n# ============================================================================\nSTATE=\"GREEN\"\nSEVERITY=\"LOW\"\nif [ \"$CONSECUTIVE\" -ge 3 ] && [ \"$PENDING_PLANS\" -ge 1 ]; then\n  STATE=\"RED\"\n  SEVERITY=\"HIGH\"\nelif [ \"$CONSECUTIVE\" -ge 3 ]; then\n  STATE=\"YELLOW\"\n  SEVERITY=\"MEDIUM\"\nfi\n\n# Cache the result regardless (so cache-hit fast-path holds for next 5 min).\n{\n  printf 'session=%s ts=%s state=%s severity=%s consecutive=%d latest_sid=%s latest_label=%s pending_plans=%d\\n' \\\n    \"$SID\" \"$TS\" \"$STATE\" \"$SEVERITY\" \"$CONSECUTIVE\" \"$LATEST_SID\" \"$LATEST_LABEL\" \"$PENDING_PLANS\"\n} > \"$CACHE\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\n# Always log a one-line summary for telemetry (passive observability).\necho \"[$TS] idle-escape-detector: state=$STATE consecutive=$CONSECUTIVE latest=$LATEST_SID(${LATEST_LABEL}) pending_plans=$PENDING_PLANS session=$SID\" \\\n  >> \"$HOOK_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\n# Idempotent fire: if marker already touched this hour-bucket, suppress system-reminder\n# emission BUT keep cache + log fresh (so observability doesn't go silent).\nif [ -f \"$MARKER\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 5: emit signal on RED/YELLOW.\n# ============================================================================\nif [ \"$STATE\" = \"GREEN\" ]; then\n  touch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Audit log entry capturing escape trigger (richer than .session-hooks.log).\n{\n  printf '[%s] %s state=%s consecutive=%d latest=%s(%s) pending_plans=%d session=%s event=%s\\n' \\\n    \"$TS\" \"ESCAPE-TRIGGER\" \"$STATE\" \"$CONSECUTIVE\" \"$LATEST_SID\" \"$LATEST_LABEL\" \"$PENDING_PLANS\" \"$SID\" \"$EVENT\"\n} >> \"$IDLE_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\nREMINDER_BODY=\"IDLE-ESCAPE-DETECTOR: state=$STATE (consecutive_idle=$CONSECUTIVE; latest=$LATEST_SID/$LATEST_LABEL; pending_plans=$PENDING_PLANS)\nPer L-S171-1 / M-S171-1 doctrine: ≥3 consecutive ROUTINE-IDLE / META-CADENCE blocks ⇒ phase-audit required BEFORE next idle.\nRequired action ONE OF:\n  (a) Dispatch phase-audit subagent (fresh-context Phase 2.5/3 status verify per verify_phase_before_next_phase doctrine);\n  (b) Open Q&A escalation bundle (human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/) if SCOPE/CHARTER ambiguity blocks substantive work;\n  (c) Pick + execute a queued session-plan from agent-workspace/session-plans/pending/ ($PENDING_PLANS available).\nForbidden: silent 4th ROUTINE-IDLE / META-CADENCE block. Charter Principle 8 + AP-5 binding.\"\n\n# UserPromptSubmit emission via Claude Code JSON contract (mirrors\n# harness-health-self-scan.sh § UserPromptSubmit emit; node may be unavailable\n# in stripped environments → fallback to stderr).\nif [ \"$EVENT\" = \"UserPromptSubmit\" ]; then\n  if command -v node >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    node -e \"process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({hookSpecificOutput:{hookEventName:'UserPromptSubmit',additionalContext:process.argv[1]}}))\" \"$REMINDER_BODY\" 2>/dev/null || \\\n      echo \"[idle-escape] $REMINDER_BODY\" >&2\n  else\n    echo \"[idle-escape] $REMINDER_BODY\" >&2\n  fi\nelif [ \"$EVENT\" = \"SessionStart\" ]; then\n  echo \"[idle-escape] $REMINDER_BODY\" >&2\nelse\n  echo \"[idle-escape] event=$EVENT $REMINDER_BODY\" >&2\nfi\n\ntouch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true\nexit 0\n","numLines":191,"startLine":1,"totalLines":191}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"501e42f6-0c58-45b2-9440-3bb1e4b1b6c0","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"22a3281c-5649-489a-a4fd-0f2f1ab6d011","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

2>/dev/null || date '+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S')\"\n43\t\n44\t# Per-session 5-min cache (perf budget).\n45\tCACHE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.phase-coherence-cache-${SID}\"\n46\tif [ -f \"$CACHE\" || [ ! -f \"$PROJ\" ]; then\n66\t  touch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true\n67\t  exit 0\n68\tfi\n69\t\n70\tset +o pipefail\n71\t\n72\t# ============================================================================\n73\t# Step 1: extract latest session Phase claim from current-execution.md.\n74\t# Headers look like:  ## S174 — Phase 3.5 — FOCUSED_IMPL-DONE: ...\n75\t# ============================================================================\n76\tLATEST_HEADER=$(grep -m 1 -E '^## S[0-9]+[a-z]?[[:space:]]+—[[:space:]]+Phase' \"$CE\" 2>/dev/null || true\n80\t  exit 0\n81\tfi\n82\t\n83\tLATEST_SID=$(echo \"$LATEST_HEADER\" | sed -E 's/^## (S[0-9]+[a-z]?).*/\\1/')\n84\tLATEST_PHASE=$(echo \"$LATEST_HEADER\" | sed -E 's/^## S[0-9]+[a-z]?[[:space:]]+—[[:space:]]+Phase[[:space:]]+([0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?).*/\\1/')\n85\t\n86\t# ============================================================================\n87\t# Step 2: collect project.md IN PROGRESS Phase rows. Tracker rows look like:\n88\t#   | 3.5 — Harness Deepening (...) | targets ... | IN PROGRESS PARTIAL-S173 | started | closed |\n89\t# We scan rows starting with `| <N>(.<M>)?` and containing \"IN PROGRESS\".\n90\t# ============================================================================\n91\tIN_PROGRESS_PHASES=$(grep -E '^\\|[[:space:]]*[0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?[[:space:]]+—' \"$PROJ\" 2>/dev/null \\\n92\t                     | grep -i 'IN PROGRESS' \\\n93\t                     | sed -E 's/^\\|[[:space:]]*([0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?)[[:space:]]+—.*/\\1/' \\\n94\t                     | sort -u)\n95\t\n96\tIN_PROGRESS_COUNT=0\n97\tif [ -n \"$IN_PROGRESS_PHASES\" ]; then\n98\t  IN_PROGRESS_COUNT=$(echo \"$IN_PROGRESS_PHASES\" | grep -c . 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n99\tfi\n100\t[[ \"$IN_PROGRESS_COUNT\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || IN_PROGRESS_COUNT=0\n101\t\n102\t# ============================================================================\n103\t# Step 3: Check (A) — latest session phase ∈ IN PROGRESS phase rows?\n104\t# ============================================================================\n105\tPHASE_MATCH=0\n106\tif [ -n \"$LATEST_PHASE\" ] && [ -n \"$IN_PROGRESS_PHASES\" ]; then\n107\t  if echo \"$IN_PROGRESS_PHASES\" | grep -qE \"^${LATEST_PHASE}$\"; then\n108\t    PHASE_MATCH=1\n109\t  fi\n110\tfi\n111\t\n112\t# ============================================================================\n113\t# Step 4: Check (B) — staleness via mtime delta + new-ADR detection.\n114\t# project.md \"Recent Architectural Decisions\" should reference ALL recent ADRs\n115\t# from agent-workspace/memory/decisions/ that are newer than the file's last\n116\t# update. If decisions/ has D-NNN files newer than project.md mtime, project.md\n117\t# is missing recent decisions → stale.\n118\t# ============================================================================\n119\tPROJ_MTIME=$(stat -c %Y \"$PROJ\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$PROJ\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n120\tCE_MTIME=$(stat -c %Y \"$CE\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$CE\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n121\tNOW=$(date +%s)\n122\tPROJ_AGE_HR=$(( (NOW - PROJ_MTIME) / 3600 ))\n123\t\n124\tNEW_ADRS=0\n125\tif [ -d \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" ] && [ \"$PROJ_MTIME\" -gt 0 ]; then\n126\t  # Count ADR files (D-NNN-*.md or NNN-*.md) newer than project.md by >2h tolerance\n127\t  # (matches project-md-staleness-check.sh tolerance).\n128\t  while IFS= read -r adr; do\n129\t    [ -f \"$adr\" ] || continue\n130\t    ADR_MTIME=$(stat -c %Y \"$adr\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$adr\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n131\t    DELTA=$(( ADR_MTIME - PROJ_MTIME ))\n132\t    if [ \"$DELTA\" -gt 7200 ]; then\n133\t      NEW_ADRS=$((NEW_ADRS + 1))\n134\t    fi\n135\t  done < <(find \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -type f \\( -name 'D-*.md' -o -name '[0-9][0-9][0-9]-*.md' \\) 2>/dev/null)\n136\tfi\n137\t\n138\t# CE is fresher than project.md by how much?\n139\tCE_PROJ_DELTA_HR=0\n140\tif [ \"$CE_MTIME\" -gt \"$PROJ_MTIME\" ] && [ \"$PROJ_MTIME\" -gt 0 ]; then\n141\t  CE_PROJ_DELTA_HR=$(( (CE_MTIME - PROJ_MTIME) / 3600 ))\n142\tfi\n143\t\n144\t# ============================================================================\n145\t# Step 5: Check (C) — at least one IN PROGRESS row exists.\n146\t# ============================================================================\n147\tNO_ACTIVE_PHASE=0\n148\tif [ \"$IN_PROGRESS_COUNT\" -eq 0 ]; then\n149\t  NO_ACTIVE_PHASE=1\n150\tfi\n151\t\n152\t# ============================================================================\n153\t# Step 6: severity classification.\n154\t# ============================================================================\n155\tSTATE=\"GREEN\"\n156\tSEVERITY=\"LOW\"\n157\tDETAILS=\"\"\n158\t\n159\tif [ -n \"$LATEST_PHASE\" ] && [ \"$IN_PROGRESS_COUNT\" -gt 0 ] && [ \"$PHASE_MATCH\" -eq 0 ]; then\n160\t  STATE=\"RED\"\n161\t  SEVERITY=\"HIGH\"\n162\t  DETAILS=\"${DETAILS}phase-mismatch(ce=${LATEST_PHASE},in-progress=$(echo \"$IN_PROGRESS_PHASES\" | tr '\\n' ',' | sed 's/,$//'));\"\n163\tfi\n164\t\n165\tif [ \"$NEW_ADRS\" -ge 3 ] && [ \"$CE_PROJ_DELTA_HR\" -gt 24 ]; M-S171-1 prevention rule #3 (companion to\n# idle-escape-detector.sh). HH-12 enhancement + HH-C.2 staleness-watchdog\n# back-stop (project-md-staleness-check.sh runs Stop-only and missed the 2-day\n# × 5-ADR slip pre-S172 — this hook fires per-prompt at UserPromptSubmit so\n# the gap surfaces sooner).\n#\n# Three checks:\n#   (A) Latest ## S<N> session header Phase claim ∈ {project.md IN PROGRESS rows}\n#   (B) project.md mtime not >48h staler than current-execution.md mtime when\n#       ADRs are referenced in current-execution.md's recent rows but absent\n#       from project.md \"Recent Architectural Decisions\" section.\n#   (C) project.md Phase Goals Tracker has at least one IN PROGRESS row when\n#       current-execution.md is being actively appended (i.e., not all phases\n#       PAUSED/DONE simultaneously while sessions still progress).\n#\n# Per CLAUDE.md hard rule + Phase 3.5 Hard Rule #2: every hook ships with companion\n# firing-test (scripts/hooks/firing-tests/phase-status-coherence-fire-test.sh).\n# Per UP-05 + L-S11-1: bash + POSIX tools only.\n# Exit 0 ALWAYS (informational, not blocking).\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nSID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-unknown}\"\nEVENT=\"${CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT:-UserPromptSubmit}\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\nCE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\"\nPROJ=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/project.md\"\nDECISIONS_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nCOHERENCE_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.phase-coherence.log\"\n\nBUCKET=\"$(date +%Y%m%d-%H 2>/dev/null)\"\n[ -z \"$BUCKET\" ] && BUCKET=\"unbucketed-$$\"\nMARKER=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.phase-coherence-fired-${BUCKET}\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date '+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S')\"\n\n# Per-session 5-min cache (perf budget).\nCACHE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.phase-coherence-cache-${SID}\"\nif [ -f \"$CACHE\" ]; then\n  CACHE_MTIME=$(stat -c %Y \"$CACHE\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$CACHE\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n  AGE=$(( $(date +%s) - CACHE_MTIME ))\n  if [ \"$AGE\" -lt 300 ]; then\n    echo \"[$TS] phase-status-coherence: CACHE-HIT age=${AGE}s session=$SID\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || true\n    exit 0\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Cleanup stale hour-bucket markers (L-S108-1 prevention).\nfor old_marker in \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"/.phase-coherence-fired-*; do\n  [ -f \"$old_marker\" ] || continue\n  case \"$old_marker\" in\n    *\".phase-coherence-fired-${BUCKET}\") ;;\n    *) rm -f \"$old_marker\" 2>/dev/null || true ;;\n  esac\ndone\n\n# Bail gracefully if either file missing.\nif [ ! -f \"$CE\" ] || [ ! -f \"$PROJ\" ]; then\n  touch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  exit 0\nfi\n\nset +o pipefail\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 1: extract latest session Phase claim from current-execution.md.\n# Headers look like:  ## S174 — Phase 3.5 — FOCUSED_IMPL-DONE: ...\n# ============================================================================\nLATEST_HEADER=$(grep -m 1 -E '^## S[0-9]+[a-z]?[[:space:]]+—[[:space:]]+Phase' \"$CE\" 2>/dev/null)\nif [ -z \"$LATEST_HEADER\" ]; then\n  touch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  echo \"session=$SID ts=$TS state=NO-HEADER detail=cannot-parse-current-execution\" > \"$CACHE\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  exit 0\nfi\n\nLATEST_SID=$(echo \"$LATEST_HEADER\" | sed -E 's/^## (S[0-9]+[a-z]?).*/\\1/')\nLATEST_PHASE=$(echo \"$LATEST_HEADER\" | sed -E 's/^## S[0-9]+[a-z]?[[:space:]]+—[[:space:]]+Phase[[:space:]]+([0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?).*/\\1/')\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 2: collect project.md IN PROGRESS Phase rows. Tracker rows look like:\n#   | 3.5 — Harness Deepening (...) | targets ... | IN PROGRESS PARTIAL-S173 | started | closed |\n# We scan rows starting with `| <N>(.<M>)?` and containing \"IN PROGRESS\".\n# ============================================================================\nIN_PROGRESS_PHASES=$(grep -E '^\\|[[:space:]]*[0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?[[:space:]]+—' \"$PROJ\" 2>/dev/null \\\n                     | grep -i 'IN PROGRESS' \\\n                     | sed -E 's/^\\|[[:space:]]*([0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?)[[:space:]]+—.*/\\1/' \\\n                     | sort -u)\n\nIN_PROGRESS_COUNT=0\nif [ -n \"$IN_PROGRESS_PHASES\" ]; then\n  IN_PROGRESS_COUNT=$(echo \"$IN_PROGRESS_PHASES\" | grep -c . 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\nfi\n[[ \"$IN_PROGRESS_COUNT\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || IN_PROGRESS_COUNT=0\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 3: Check (A) — latest session phase ∈ IN PROGRESS phase rows?\n# ============================================================================\nPHASE_MATCH=0\nif [ -n \"$LATEST_PHASE\" ] && [ -n \"$IN_PROGRESS_PHASES\" ]; then\n  if echo \"$IN_PROGRESS_PHASES\" | grep -qE \"^${LATEST_PHASE}$\"; then\n    PHASE_MATCH=1\n  fi\nfi\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 4: Check (B) — staleness via mtime delta + new-ADR detection.\n# project.md \"Recent Architectural Decisions\" should reference ALL recent ADRs\n# from agent-workspace/memory/decisions/ that are newer than the file's last\n# update. If decisions/ has D-NNN files newer than project.md mtime, project.md\n# is missing recent decisions → stale.\n# ============================================================================\nPROJ_MTIME=$(stat -c %Y \"$PROJ\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$PROJ\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\nCE_MTIME=$(stat -c %Y \"$CE\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$CE\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\nNOW=$(date +%s)\nPROJ_AGE_HR=$(( (NOW - PROJ_MTIME) / 3600 ))\n\nNEW_ADRS=0\nif [ -d \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" ] && [ \"$PROJ_MTIME\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  # Count ADR files (D-NNN-*.md or NNN-*.md) newer than project.md by >2h tolerance\n  # (matches project-md-staleness-check.sh tolerance).\n  while IFS= read -r adr; do\n    [ -f \"$adr\" ] || continue\n    ADR_MTIME=$(stat -c %Y \"$adr\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$adr\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n    DELTA=$(( ADR_MTIME - PROJ_MTIME ))\n    if [ \"$DELTA\" -gt 7200 ]; then\n      NEW_ADRS=$((NEW_ADRS + 1))\n    fi\n  done < <(find \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -type f \\( -name 'D-*.md' -o -name '[0-9][0-9][0-9]-*.md' \\) 2>/dev/null)\nfi\n\n# CE is fresher than project.md by how much?\nCE_PROJ_DELTA_HR=0\nif [ \"$CE_MTIME\" -gt \"$PROJ_MTIME\" ] && [ \"$PROJ_MTIME\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  CE_PROJ_DELTA_HR=$(( (CE_MTIME - PROJ_MTIME) / 3600 ))\nfi\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 5: Check (C) — at least one IN PROGRESS row exists.\n# ============================================================================\nNO_ACTIVE_PHASE=0\nif [ \"$IN_PROGRESS_COUNT\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  NO_ACTIVE_PHASE=1\nfi\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 6: severity classification.\n# ============================================================================\nSTATE=\"GREEN\"\nSEVERITY=\"LOW\"\nDETAILS=\"\"\n\nif [ -n \"$LATEST_PHASE\" ] && [ \"$IN_PROGRESS_COUNT\" -gt 0 ] && [ \"$PHASE_MATCH\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  STATE=\"RED\"\n  SEVERITY=\"HIGH\"\n  DETAILS=\"${DETAILS}phase-mismatch(ce=${LATEST_PHASE},in-progress=$(echo \"$IN_PROGRESS_PHASES\" | tr '\\n' ',' | sed 's/,$//'));\"\nfi\n\nif [ \"$NEW_ADRS\" -ge 3 ] && [ \"$CE_PROJ_DELTA_HR\" -gt 24 ]; then\n  if [ \"$STATE\" = \"GREEN\" ]; then\n    STATE=\"YELLOW\"\n    SEVERITY=\"MEDIUM\"\n  fi\n  DETAILS=\"${DETAILS}stale-project-md(new_adrs=${NEW_ADRS},ce_delta_hr=${CE_PROJ_DELTA_HR});\"\nfi\n\nif [ \"$NO_ACTIVE_PHASE\" -eq 1 ]; then\n  if [ \"$STATE\" = \"GREEN\" ]; then\n    STATE=\"YELLOW\"\n    SEVERITY=\"MEDIUM\"\n  fi\n  DETAILS=\"${DETAILS}no-in-progress-phase(rows=0);\"\nfi\n\n# Cache result.\n{\n  printf 'session=%s ts=%s state=%s severity=%s ce_phase=%s in_progress=%s match=%d new_adrs=%d ce_delta_hr=%d details=%s\\n' \\\n    \"$SID\" \"$TS\" \"$STATE\" \"$SEVERITY\" \"$LATEST_PHASE\" \"$(echo \"$IN_PROGRESS_PHASES\" | tr '\\n' ',' | sed 's/,$//')\" \\\n    \"$PHASE_MATCH\" \"$NEW_ADRS\" \"$CE_PROJ_DELTA_HR\" \"$DETAILS\"\n} > \"$CACHE\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\necho \"[$TS] phase-status-coherence: state=$STATE ce_sid=$LATEST_SID ce_phase=$LATEST_PHASE in_progress=[$(echo \"$IN_PROGRESS_PHASES\" | tr '\\n' ',' | sed 's/,$//')] match=$PHASE_MATCH new_adrs=$NEW_ADRS session=$SID\" \\\n  >> \"$HOOK_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\n# Idempotent fire — suppress system-reminder if already fired this hour-bucket.\nif [ -f \"$MARKER\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\nif [ \"$STATE\" = \"GREEN\" ]; then\n  touch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Audit log entry.\n{\n  printf '[%s] %s state=%s severity=%s ce_sid=%s ce_phase=%s in_progress=[%s] match=%d new_adrs=%d ce_delta_hr=%d details=%s session=%s event=%s\\n' \\\n    \"$TS\" \"DRIFT-DETECTED\" \"$STATE\" \"$SEVERITY\" \"$LATEST_SID\" \"$LATEST_PHASE\" \\\n    \"$(echo \"$IN_PROGRESS_PHASES\" | tr '\\n' ',' | sed 's/,$//')\" \\\n    \"$PHASE_MATCH\" \"$NEW_ADRS\" \"$CE_PROJ_DELTA_HR\" \"$DETAILS\" \"$SID\" \"$EVENT\"\n} >> \"$COHERENCE_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\nREMINDER_BODY=\"PHASE-STATUS-COHERENCE: state=$STATE severity=$SEVERITY\nLatest current-execution.md session: $LATEST_SID — Phase $LATEST_PHASE\nproject.md Phase Goals Tracker IN PROGRESS rows: [$(echo \"$IN_PROGRESS_PHASES\" | tr '\\n' ',' | sed 's/,$//')]\nMatch: $PHASE_MATCH (0=mismatch, 1=aligned)\nNew ADRs in decisions/ since project.md last update: $NEW_ADRS (delta_hr=$CE_PROJ_DELTA_HR)\nDetails: $DETAILS\n\nPer HH-12 + HH-C.2 doctrine + verify_phase_before_next_phase BINDING: silent phase advance is forbidden.\nRequired action ONE OF:\n  (a) Reconcile project.md Phase Goals Tracker against current-execution.md (add missing IN PROGRESS row, adjust DONE/PAUSED status, update Recent ADRs);\n  (b) Correct current-execution.md if the latest session block claims a Phase that is not actually active;\n  (c) Run /drift-check or fresh-context audit subagent if the source-of-truth is genuinely ambiguous.\nForbidden: continue substantive work until reconciled. Exit 0 ALWAYS (informational, not blocking).\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nSID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-unknown}\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nHEALTH_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.harness-health.log\"\nCACHE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.harness-health-cache-${SID}\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\n\n# === Same-session cache check (5-min TTL) ===\nif [ -f \"$CACHE\" ]; then\n  CACHE_MTIME=$(stat -c %Y \"$CACHE\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$CACHE\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n  AGE=$(( $(date +%s) - CACHE_MTIME ))\n  if [ \"$AGE\" -lt 300 ]; then\n    echo \"[$TS] harness-health-self-scan: CACHE-HIT age=${AGE}s session=$SID\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n    exit 0\n  fi\nfi\n\n# === Aggregation counters ===\nHIGH=0\nMEDIUM=0\nLOW=0\nSKIP=0\nFAIL_DETAILS=\"\"\n\nemit_fail() {\n  local signal=\"$1\" sev=\"$2\" detail=\"$3\"\n  case \"$sev\" in\n    HIGH) HIGH=$((HIGH + 1)) ;;\n    MEDIUM) MEDIUM=$((MEDIUM + 1)) ;;\n    LOW) LOW=$((LOW + 1)) ;;\n  esac\n  FAIL_DETAILS=\"${FAIL_DETAILS}${signal}=${sev}(${detail});\"\n  printf '[%s] %s sev=%s detail=%s session=%s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$signal\" \"$sev\" \"$detail\" \"$SID\" >> \"$HEALTH_LOG\"\n}\n\nemit_skip() {\n  SKIP=$((SKIP + 1))\n  printf '[%s] %s SKIP=%s session=%s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$1\" \"$2\" \"$SID\" >> \"$HEALTH_LOG\"\n}\n\n# === HH-1: Stop hook fires ≥1/session (KI-S49b-1 suppress: HIGH→MEDIUM) ===\nHH1_check() {\n  [ ! -f \"$HOOK_LOG\" ] && { emit_skip \"HH-1\" \"log-missing\"; return; }\n  local start_line stop_count\n  start_line=$(grep -n \"SessionStart session=$SID\" \"$HOOK_LOG\" 2>/dev/null | tail -1 | cut -d: -f1)\n  [ -z \"$start_line\" ] && { emit_skip \"HH-1\" \"no-SessionStart-for-SID\"; return; }\n  stop_count=$(awk \"NR>$start_line\" \"$HOOK_LOG\" 2>/dev/null | grep -c \"Stop session=$SID\" || echo 0)\n  [[ \"$stop_count\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || stop_count=0\n  if [ \"$stop_count\" -lt 1 ]; then\n    # KI-S49b-1 suppression check\n    local sev=\"HIGH\"\n    [ -f \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.harness-health-ki-S49b-1\" ] && sev=\"MEDIUM\"\n    emit_fail \"HH-1-STOP-NOT-FIRING\" \"$sev\" \"stop_count=0 (Windows quirk M-S49b-1)\"\n  fi\n}\n\n# === HH-2: UserPromptSubmit ≥1 fire in last 10 min ===\nHH2_check() {\n  [ ! -f \"$HOOK_LOG\" ] && { emit_skip \"HH-2\" \"log-missing\"; return; }\n  local cutoff recent\n  cutoff=$(date -d \"10 min ago\" -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || python3 -c \"import datetime; print((datetime.datetime.now()-datetime.timedelta(minutes=10)).isoformat())\" 2>/dev/null || echo \"\")\n  [ -z \"$cutoff\" ] && { emit_skip \"HH-2\" \"date-math-unavail\"; return; }\n  recent=$(awk -v cutoff=\"[$cutoff\" '$0 > cutoff' \"$HOOK_LOG\" 2>/dev/null | grep -c \"UserPromptSubmit\" || echo 0)\n  [[ \"$recent\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || recent=0\n  if [ \"$recent\" -lt 1 ]; then\n    emit_fail \"HH-2-USERPROMPT-NOT-FIRING\" \"HIGH\" \"recent_10min=0\"\n  fi\n}\n\n# === HH-3: Promote-rule cycle delta < 10 days ===\nHH3_check() {\n  local prom_dir=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/observations\"\n  [ ! -d \"$prom_dir\" ] && { emit_skip \"HH-3\" \"obs-dir-missing\"; return; }\n  local latest age_days\n  latest=$(find \"$prom_dir\" -maxdepth 1 -name 'promote-rule-S*.md' -type f 2>/dev/null | xargs -r ls -t 2>/dev/null | head -1)\n  [ -z \"$latest\" ] && { emit_fail \"HH-3-PROMOTE-RULE-MISSING\" \"MEDIUM\" \"no-promote-rule-observation-found\"; return; }\n  local mtime\n  mtime=$(stat -c %Y \"$latest\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$latest\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n  age_days=$(( ( $(date +%s) - mtime ) / 86400 ))\n  if [ \"$age_days\" -gt 14 ]; then\n    emit_fail \"HH-3-PROMOTE-RULE-STALE\" \"HIGH\" \"age_days=$age_days\"\n  elif [ \"$age_days\" -gt 10 ]; then\n    emit_fail \"HH-3-PROMOTE-RULE-STALE\" \"MEDIUM\" \"age_days=$age_days\"\n  fi\n}\n\n# === HH-4: Auto-detect orphans ≤ 2 ===\nHH4_check() {\n  local notes=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md\"\n  [ ! -f \"$notes\" ] && { emit_skip \"HH-4\" \"agent-notes-missing\"; return; }\n  local candidates hooks orphan_delta\n  candidates=$(grep -c \"Auto-detect:.*yes\" \"$notes\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n  hooks=$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks\" -maxdepth 1 -name '*.sh' -type f 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\n  [[ \"$candidates\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || candidates=0\n  [[ \"$hooks\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || hooks=0\n  orphan_delta=$(( candidates > hooks ? candidates - hooks : 0 ))\n  if [ \"$orphan_delta\" -gt 2 ]; then\n    emit_fail \"HH-4-AUTO-DETECT-ORPHAN\" \"MEDIUM\" \"delta=$orphan_delta candidates=$candidates hooks=$hooks\"\n  fi\n}\n\n# === HH-5: Tier 1 bloat (defer to existing hook) ===\nHH5_check() {\n  local hook=\"$PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks/tier1-bloat-check.sh\"\n  [ ! -f \"$hook\" ] && { emit_skip \"HH-5\" \"tier1-bloat-check-missing\"; return; }\n  if ! bash \"$hook\" >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    emit_fail \"HH-5-TIER1-BLOAT\" \"HIGH\" \"tier1-bloat-check-rc-nonzero\"\n  fi\n}\n\n# === HH-6: dispatch-pending JSONL no stale `state=pending` > 2hr ===\nHH6_check() {\n  local jsonl_dir=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n  local two_hr_ago\n  two_hr_ago=$(( $(date +%s) - 7200 ))\n  local stale=0\n  for f in \"$jsonl_dir\"/.dispatch-pending-*.jsonl; do\n    [ -f \"$f\" ] || continue\n    local mtime\n    mtime=$(stat -c %Y \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n    [ \"$mtime\" -gt \"$two_hr_ago\" ] && continue\n    if tail -1 \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -q '\"state\":\"pending\"'; then\n      stale=$((stale + 1))\n    fi\n  done\n  if [ \"$stale\" -ge 3 ]; then\n    emit_fail \"HH-6-DISPATCH-STALE\" \"HIGH\" \"stale=$stale\"\n  elif [ \"$stale\" -ge 1 ]; then\n    emit_fail \"HH-6-DISPATCH-STALE\" \"MEDIUM\" \"stale=$stale\"\n  fi\n}\n\n# === HH-7: Checkpoint freshness ===\nHH7_check() {\n  local latest=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md\"\n  [ ! -f \"$latest\" ] && { emit_fail \"HH-7-CHECKPOINT-MISSING\" \"MEDIUM\" \"latest.md-not-found\"; return; }\n  local now mtime age_hr\n  now=$(date +%s)\n  mtime=$(stat -c %Y \"$latest\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$latest\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n  age_hr=$(( (now - mtime) / 3600 ))\n  local sess_dir=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/sessions\"\n  local latest_sess sess_mtime sess_delta_hr\n  latest_sess=$(find \"$sess_dir\" -maxdepth 1 -name '*.md' -type f 2>/dev/null | xargs -r ls -t 2>/dev/null | head -1)\n  if [ -n \"$latest_sess\" ]; then\n    sess_mtime=$(stat -c %Y \"$latest_sess\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$latest_sess\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n    sess_delta_hr=$(( (now - sess_mtime) / 3600 ))\n  else\n    sess_delta_hr=0\n  fi\n  # PASS: checkpoint within 24hr OR within session-mtime + 2hr window\n  if [ \"$age_hr\" -gt 24 ] && [ \"$age_hr\" -gt \"$(( sess_delta_hr + 2 ))\" ]; then\n    emit_fail \"HH-7-CHECKPOINT-STALE\" \"MEDIUM\" \"age_hr=$age_hr sess_delta_hr=$sess_delta_hr\"\n  fi\n}\n\n# === HH-8: Charter md5 unchanged in non-ratify session ===\nHH8_check() {\n  local charter=\"$PROJECT_DIR/PROJECT_CHARTER.md\"\n  local baseline_file=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.charter-md5-baseline\"\n  [ ! -f \"$charter\" ] && { emit_fail \"HH-8-CHARTER-MISSING\" \"HIGH\" \"charter-not-found\"; return; }\n  if [ ! -f \"$baseline_file\" ]; then\n    # First-run: write baseline + skip\n    md5sum \"$charter\" 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $1}' > \"$baseline_file\" 2>/dev/null || \\\n      md5 \"$charter\" 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $NF}' > \"$baseline_file\" 2>/dev/null || true\n    emit_skip \"HH-8\" \"baseline-initialized\"\n    return\n  fi\n  local current_md5 baseline_md5 ratify_marker\n  current_md5=$(md5sum \"$charter\" 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $1}' || md5 \"$charter\" 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $NF}' || echo \"\")\n  baseline_md5=$(cat \"$baseline_file\" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')\n  ratify_marker=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.charter-ratify-active-${SID}\"\n  if [ \"$current_md5\" != \"$baseline_md5\" ] && [ ! -f \"$ratify_marker\" ]; then\n    emit_fail \"HH-8-CHARTER-DRIFT\" \"HIGH\" \"md5-changed-without-ratify-marker\"\n  fi\n}\n\n# === HH-9: mistake-log freshness OR explicit \"no mistakes\" ===\nHH9_check() {\n  local sess_dir=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/sessions\"\n  local mistake=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/mistake-log.md\"\n  local latest_sess sess_mtime mistake_mtime delta\n  latest_sess=$(find \"$sess_dir\" -maxdepth 1 -name '*.md' -type f 2>/dev/null | xargs -r ls -t 2>/dev/null | head -1)\n  [ -z \"$latest_sess\" ] && { emit_skip \"HH-9\" \"no-session-log\"; return; }\n  sess_mtime=$(stat -c %Y \"$latest_sess\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$latest_sess\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n  mistake_mtime=$(stat -c %Y \"$mistake\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$mistake\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n  delta=$(( sess_mtime > mistake_mtime ? sess_mtime - mistake_mtime : mistake_mtime - sess_mtime ))\n  if [ \"$delta\" -le 300 ]; then return; fi\n  if grep -qiE \"no mistakes (this )?(session|turn)|mistakes new.*none\" \"$latest_sess\" 2>/dev/null; then return; fi\n  emit_fail \"HH-9-MISTAKE-LOG-UNFRESH\" \"MEDIUM\" \"delta_sec=$delta no-explicit-declaration\"\n}\n\n# === HH-10: Firing-test orphans ≤ 2 ===\nHH10_check() {\n  local hooks_dir=\"$PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks\"\n  local tests_dir=\"$hooks_dir/firing-tests\"\n  [ ! -d \"$tests_dir\" ] && { emit_fail \"HH-10-FIRING-TESTS-DIR-MISSING\" \"MEDIUM\" \"no-firing-tests-dir\"; return; }\n  local orphans=0 hook name test\n  for hook in \"$hooks_dir\"/*.sh; do\n    [ -f \"$hook\" ] || continue\n    name=$(basename \"$hook\" .sh)\n    test=\"$tests_dir/${name}-fire-test.sh\"\n    [ ! -f \"$test\" ] && orphans=$((orphans + 1))\n  done\n  if [ \"$orphans\" -gt 2 ]; then\n    emit_fail \"HH-10-FIRING-TEST-ORPHAN\" \"MEDIUM\" \"orphans=$orphans\"\n  fi\n}\n\n# === HH-11: .session-hooks.log mtime < 60 min ===\nHH11_check() {\n  [ ! -f \"$HOOK_LOG\" ] && { emit_fail \"HH-11-LOG-MISSING\" \"HIGH\" \"session-hooks-log-not-found\"; return; }\n  local now mtime age_min\n  now=$(date +%s)\n  mtime=$(stat -c %Y \"$HOOK_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$HOOK_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n  age_min=$(( (now - mtime) / 60 ))\n  if [ \"$age_min\" -gt 60 ]; then\n    emit_fail \"HH-11-LOG-FROZEN\" \"HIGH\" \"age_min=$age_min\"\n  fi\n}\n\n# === HH-12: current-execution.md Phase = project.md Phase ===\nHH12_check() {\n  local ce=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\"\n  local proj=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/project.md\"\n  if [ ! -f \"$ce\" ] || [ ! -f \"$proj\" ]; then\n    emit_skip \"HH-12\" \"missing-files\"\n    return\n  fi\n  local ce_phase proj_phase\n  ce_phase=$(grep -m1 -E '^## S[0-9]+ — Phase' \"$ce\" 2>/dev/null | grep -oE 'Phase [0-9.]+' | head -1)\n  proj_phase=$(grep -E '^\\| [0-9.]+ —' \"$proj\" 2>/dev/null | grep -i 'IN PROGRESS' | head -1 | grep -oE 'Phase [0-9.]+' | head -1)\n  if [ -z \"$ce_phase\" ] || [ -z \"$proj_phase\" ]; then\n    emit_skip \"HH-12\" \"phase-parse-failed\"\n    return\n  fi\n  if [ \"$ce_phase\" != \"$proj_phase\" ]; then\n    emit_fail \"HH-12-PHASE-DRIFT\" \"HIGH\" \"ce=$ce_phase proj=$proj_phase\"\n  fi\n}\n\n# === Run all signals (cheap-first ordering) ===\nHH7_check   # checkpoint mtime — very cheap\nHH11_check  # log mtime — very cheap\nHH8_check   # md5 — cheap\nHH1_check   # grep log — cheap-medium\nHH2_check   # awk + grep — medium\nHH9_check   # mtime + grep session log — medium\nHH3_check   # find + xargs ls — medium\nHH6_check   # multi-file mtime + tail — medium\nHH4_check   # grep agent-notes — medium\nHH10_check  # find loop — medium\nHH5_check   # delegate to existing hook — medium-heavy\nHH12_check  # multi-file grep — heavy\n\n# === Aggregation + emission ===\nTOTAL=$(( HIGH + MEDIUM + LOW ))\nSTATE=\"GREEN\"\nif [ \"$HIGH\" -ge 2 ] || { [ \"$HIGH\" -ge 1 ] && [ \"$MEDIUM\" -ge 2 ]; }; then\n  STATE=\"RED-2\"\nelif [ \"$HIGH\" -ge 1 ]; then\n  STATE=\"RED-1\"\nelif [ \"$MEDIUM\" -ge 1 ]; then\n  STATE=\"YELLOW\"\nfi\n\n# Cache the result\n{\n  printf 'session=%s ts=%s state=%s high=%d medium=%d low=%d skip=%d details=%s\\n' \\\n    \"$SID\" \"$TS\" \"$STATE\" \"$HIGH\" \"$MEDIUM\" \"$LOW\" \"$SKIP\" \"$FAIL_DETAILS\"\n} > \"$CACHE\"\n\n# Emit summary to hook log\necho \"[$TS] harness-health-self-scan: state=$STATE high=$HIGH medium=$MEDIUM low=$LOW skip=$SKIP session=$SID\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n\n# Emit system-reminder ONLY if non-GREEN AND running under UserPromptSubmit (CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT env or similar)\n# We detect UserPromptSubmit by checking if stdin is a JSON payload (UserPromptSubmit feeds JSON).\nEVENT=\"${CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT:-unknown}\"\nif [ \"$STATE\" != \"GREEN\" ] && [ \"$EVENT\" = \"UserPromptSubmit\" ]; then\n  REMINDER=\"HARNESS-HEALTH-SELF-SCAN: state=$STATE (high=$HIGH medium=$MEDIUM)\nFailures: $FAIL_DETAILS\nAction: review FAILs in agent-workspace/memory/.harness-health.log; remediate per harness-health-protocol.md § Pass/Fail Aggregation Rules. Exit code 0 (informational).\"\n  node -e \"\nprocess.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({\n  hookSpecificOutput: {\n    hookEventName: 'UserPromptSubmit',\n    additionalContext: process.argv[1]\n  }\n}));\n\" \"$REMINDER\" 2>/dev/null || true\nfi\n\n# SessionStart: emit to stderr (visible in session-hooks.log per existing convention)\nif [ \"$STATE\" != \"GREEN\" ] && [ \"$EVENT\" = \"SessionStart\" ]; then\n  echo \"[harness-health] state=$STATE high=$HIGH medium=$MEDIUM details=$FAIL_DETAILS\" >&2\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","numLines":309,"startLine":1,"totalLines":309}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b8268a77-0645-4a20-8a27-f57a9c92c36d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"22a3281c-5649-489a-a4fd-0f2f1ab6d011","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}
