L-S10-1: if/then/fi only; split-local.\n17\tset -uo pipefail\n18\ttrap 'exit 0' ERR\n19\t\n20\tPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\n21\tHOOKS_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks\"\n22\tLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\n23\tNOTIF_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\n24\tTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)\"\n25\t\n26\tmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\" 2>/dev/null || true\n27\t\n28\tVIOLATIONS=0\n29\tVIOLATION_LIST=\"\"\n30\t\n31\temit() {\n32\t  local code=\"$1\" file=\"$2\" detail=\"$3\"\n33\t  VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n34\t  VIOLATION_LIST=\"${VIOLATION_LIST}  - ${code}: ${file} — ${detail}\"$'\\n'\n35\t}\n36\t\n37\t# === Check 1: L-S11-1 Phase 0 portability — no python/jq/yq/pip/npm in scripts/hooks ===\n38\t# Exempt: comments, strings, and explicit Phase-1+ stubs marked with \"# PHASE-1+\" guard comment.\n39\tif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n40\t  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n41\t    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n42\t    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n43\t    # Skip self.\n44\t    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n45\t    # Strip comments + heredocs roughly: scan only \"executable\" lines (not # comments).\n46\t    NON_COMMENT=\"$(grep -vE '^[[:space:]]*#' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n47\t    # Look for forbidden tooling invocations as command tokens.\n48\t    if printf '%s' \"$NON_COMMENT\" | grep -qE '(^|[[:space:]`(|&;])(python3?|jq|yq|pip|pip3|npm|pnpm)([[:space:]]|$)'; then\n49\t      emit \"L-S11-1-PORTABILITY\" \"$bn\" \"non-Phase-0-portable invocation (python/jq/yq/pip/npm) — Phase 0 doctrine = bash + POSIX only\"\n50\t    fi\n51\t  done\n52\tfi\n53\t\n54\t# === Check 4: L-S43b-9 printf format-string starting with `-` needs `--` sentinel ===\n55\t# `printf '-foo'` interprets `-foo` as an option flag, not as the format string. Fix is\n56\t# `printf -- '-foo'`. Encountered 2x during HR-3 deploy per L-S43b-9. False-positive-safe:\n57\t# matches only lines where printf is followed by a literal quoted dash, AND the same line\n58\t# does NOT also contain `printf --` (which means the sentinel is already present).\n59\tif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n60\t  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n61\t    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n62\t    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n63\t    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n64\t    BAD=\"$(grep -nE \"printf[[:space:]]+['\\\"]-\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -vE \"printf[[:space:]]+--\" || true)\"\n65\t    if [ -n \"$BAD\" ]; then\n66\t      emit \"L-S43b-9-PRINTF-DASH\" \"$bn\" \"printf format starts with '-' without '--' sentinel — use 'printf -- ...'\"\n67\t    fi\n68\t  done\n69\tfi\n70\t\n71\t# === Check 2: L-S13-1 producer-consumer log path mismatch (silent telemetry brick) ===\n72\t# Heuristic: declared LOG-like variable but never used as redirect target within same file.\n73\tif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n74\t  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n75\t    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n76\t    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n77\t    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n78\t    # Extract variable names matching *LOG*|*FILE*|*OUTPUT*|*PATH* pattern that get assigned a path-looking value.\n79\t    DECL_VARS=\"$(grep -oE '^[A-Z_]+(LOG|FILE|OUTPUT|PATH|DIR)[A-Z_]*=' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | sed 's/=$//' | sort -u || true)\"\n80\t    for v in $DECL_VARS; do\n81\t      [ -z \"$v\" ] && continue\n82\t      # Skip universally-known env vars that are inputs not outputs.\n83\t      case \"$v\" in\n84\t        PROJECT_DIR|HOOKS_DIR|CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR|HOOK_LOG|LOOP_DIR|DOGFOOD_DIR|NOTIF_DIR|EVENTS_DIR|INDEX_DIR|ARCHIVE_DIR) continue ;;\n85\t      esac\n86\t      # Check for \"$VAR\" or ${VAR} appearing on right side of >> or > redirect, OR as `tee $VAR`.\n87\t      if ! grep -qE \"(>>?|tee[[:space:]]+(-a[[:space:]]+)?)[[:space:]]*[\\\"]?\\\\\\$\\\\{?${v}\\\\}?\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n88\t        # Also accept if VAR is read FROM, or is a path argument to common command (find/cd/test/ls/mkdir/cp/mv/echo/printf/...)\n89\t        if ! grep -qE \"[\\\"]?\\\\\\$\\\\{?${v}\\\\}?\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -qvE \"^[A-Z_]+=\"; then\n90\t          # Final fallback: any non-assignment line referencing the var counts as \"used\".\n91\t          if ! grep -vE \"^[[:space:]]*${v}=\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -qE \"[\\\"]?\\\\\\$\\\\{?${v}\\\\}?[\\\"]?\"; then\n92\t            emit \"L-S13-1-ORPHAN-LOG-VAR\" \"$bn\" \"variable \\$${v} declared but never referenced after assignment\"\n93\t          fi\n94\t        fi\n95\t      fi\n96\t    done\n97\t  done\n98\tfi\n99\t\n100\t# === Check 3: D-IDENTITY S16 #10 — fabricated SUPERVISED-mode regression in LIVE config ===\n101\t# Per S15-close user correction (CHARTER-tier): full autonomous is the ONLY mode; \"SUPERVISED\"\n102\t# was a fabricated default-until-Track-7 framing that was never user-authorized. Scan LIVE\n103\t# config files; flag any `SUPERVISED|autonomous_mode:\\s*false|until Track 7` regression.\n104\t# Exempt historical session/checkpoint/decision/Q&A files (they record past state legitimately).\n105\tLIVE_TARGETS=(\n106\t  \"$PROJECT_DIR/.claude/settings.json\"\n107\t  \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\"\n108\t  \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/project.md\"\n109\t  \"$PROJECT_DIR/CLAUDE.md\"\n110\t  \"$PROJECT_DIR/AGENT_OPERATING_MANUAL.md\"\n111\t)\n112\t# Live constitution + proposals (excluding archived/superseded versions).\n113\tif [ -d \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/constitution\" ]; then\n114\t  while IFS= read -r -d '' p; do LIVE_TARGETS+=(\"$p\"); done < <(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/constitution\" -maxdepth 1 -name '*.md' -type f -print0 2>/dev/null)\n115\tfi\n116\tif [ -d \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/proposals\" ]; then\n117\t  while IFS= read -r -d '' p; do LIVE_TARGETS+=(\"$p\"); done < <(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/proposals\" -maxdepth 1 -name '*.md' -type f -print0 2>/dev/null)\n118\tfi\n119\t\n120\tREGRESSION_PATTERN='SUPERVISED[[:space:]]*mode|autonomous_mode:[[:space:]]*false|until[[:space:]]+Track[[:space:]]+7|SUPERVISED-only'\n121\tfor t in \"${LIVE_TARGETS[@]}\"; do\n122\t  [ ! -f \"$t\" ] && continue\n123\t  rel=\"${t#$PROJECT_DIR/}\"\n124\t  # Scan; deny pattern matches but allow file to legitimately quote/document the rule itself.\n125\t  if grep -qE \"$REGRESSION_PATTERN\" \"$t\" 2>/dev/null; then\n126\t    # Allow if the line ALSO contains a denial marker OR a negation-form of the regression\n127\t    # pattern (i.e. file is documenting the prohibition, not violating it). L-S69-1: artifact-\n128\t    # verifier hooks must whitelist legitimate canonical phrasings of the rule they enforce.\n129\t    HITS=\"$(grep -nE \"$REGRESSION_PATTERN\" \"$t\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n130\t    while IFS= read -r line; do\n131\t      [ -z \"$line\" ] && continue\n132\t      if printf '%s' \"$line\" | grep -qiE 'fabricated|deprecated|anti-pattern|never user-authorized|do NOT|MUST NOT|forbidden|not user-authorized|no[[:space:]]+[\"]?(SUPERVISED|until[[:space:]]+Track|human-in-the-loop|autonomous_mode)|D-IDENTITY|bash-hook-lint'; then\n133\t        continue\n134\t      fi\n135\t      emit \"D-IDENTITY-AUTONOMOUS-REGRESSION\" \"$rel\" \"live-config line matches forbidden pattern: $(printf '%s' \"$line\" | cut -c1-100)\"\n136\t      break\n137\t    done <<< \"$HITS\"\n138\t  fi\n139\tdone\n140\t\n141\t# === Check 5: M-S51-1 grep-on-imagined-format anti-pattern (Pattern A) ===\n142\t# Origin: S51 T3.1 — promotion-cycle-trigger.sh greped literal `^\\*\\*Session N\\*\\*:` against\n143\t# real `## SXX —` header format → 0 matches → silent broken hook for ~12hr.\n144\t# Detection: literal \"Session N\" (capital-N placeholder; never a real header) appearing\n145\t# inside any grep/sed/awk argument. Also catches the variant with escaped asterisks.\n146\tif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n147\t  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n148\t    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n149\t    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n150\t    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n151\t    BAD_A=\"$(grep -nE \"(grep|sed|awk)[[:space:]].*Session N([^a-zA-Z0-9]|$)\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -vE \"^[[:space:]]*[0-9]+:[[:space:]]*#\" || true)\"\n152\t    if [ -n \"$BAD_A\" ]; then\n153\t      emit \"M-S51-1-IMAGINED-FORMAT\" \"$bn\" \"literal 'Session N' placeholder in grep/sed/awk pattern — verify against real current-execution.md format (L-S51-1)\"\n154\t    fi\n155\t  done\n156\tfi\n157\t\n158\t# === Check 6: L-S48m-1 CLAUDE_SESSION_ID empty-on-Windows anti-pattern (Pattern B) ===\n159\t# Origin: S48m profile-template-auto-populate.sh — marker filename used $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID\n160\t# without :- fallback; on Windows the env var is empty → marker collides on every fire →\n161\t# silent skip after first fire. L-S48m-1 fix: use session-log basename instead.\n162\tif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n163\t  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n164\t    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n165\t    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n166\t    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n167\t    BAD_B=\"$(grep -nE \"[\\\"]?\\\\.[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+(-fired|-marker|-ran|-flag)-\\\\\\$\\\\{?CLAUDE_SESSION_ID\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -vE \"^[[:space:]]*[0-9]+:[[:space:]]*#\" || true)\"\n168\t    if [ -n \"$BAD_B\" ]; then\n169\t      emit \"L-S48m-1-CLAUDE-SESSION-ID-MARKER\" \"$bn\" \"marker filename uses \\$CLAUDE_SESSION_ID (empty on Windows; silent skip — L-S48m-1)\"\n170\t    fi\n171\t  done\n172\tfi\n173\t\n174\t# === Check 6b: L-S108-1 CLAUDE_SESSION_ID fallback-to-constant anti-pattern (Pattern B') ===\n175\t# Origin: S108 qa-pending-auto-mover.sh — SID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-main}\" then\n176\t# MARKER=\"...-${SID}\". CLAUDE_SESSION_ID empty on Windows → fallback \"main\" shared across\n177\t# ALL sessions → marker filename collides → permanent idempotency lockout (M-S108-1: bundle\n178\t# stuck in pending/ for 24+h). Check 6 only catches DIRECT $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID in marker;\n179\t# misses INDIRECT-via-VAR form. Fix: use date hour-bucket or session-log basename.\n180\t#\n181\t# Detection: file has BOTH (a) fallback-to-constant assignment AND (b) any marker filename\n182\t# pattern using $VAR. The combination indicates likely indirect-via-VAR lockout.\n183\tif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n184\t  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n185\t    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n186\t    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n187\t    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n188\t    # (a) Suspicious assignment: ${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-WORD} where WORD is alphabetic\n189\t    HAS_FALLBACK=\"$(grep -nE '\\$\\{CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9_-]*\\}' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -vE \"^[[:space:]]*[0-9]+:[[:space:]]*#\" || true)\"\n190\t    if [ -n \"$HAS_FALLBACK\" ]; then\n191\t      # (b) Marker filename pattern using ANY $VAR (dot-prefixed -fired/-marker/-ran/-flag/-written/-pending suffix)\n192\t      HAS_MARKER=\"$(grep -nE '\\.[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+-(fired|marker|ran|flag|written|pending)-\\$\\{?[A-Z_]' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -vE \"^[[:space:]]*[0-9]+:[[:space:]]*#\" || true)\"\n193\t      if [ -n \"$HAS_MARKER\" ]; then\n194\t        emit \"L-S108-1-CLAUDE-SESSION-ID-FALLBACK-CONSTANT\" \"$bn\" \"fallback-to-constant \\${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-WORD} + marker filename present (likely per-session lockout on Windows; use date hour-bucket or session-log basename — L-S108-1)\"\n195\t      fi\n196\t    fi\n197\t  done\n198\tfi\n199\t\n200\t# === Check 7: L-S48d-1 pipefail+ERR-trap+bare-grep anti-pattern (Pattern C) — REFINED S58 ===\n201\t# Origin: S48d profile-template-auto-populate.sh — set -uo pipefail + trap 'exit 0' ERR\n202\t# + bare `grep -m1 ...` returning nonzero on no-match → ERR trap fires silently → script\n203\t# exits 0 with NO log line → invisible failure. Fix: wrap each grep with `|| true` or\n204\t# convert to `if grep ...; then ... fi` form.\n205\t#\n206\t# S54 refinement: caught command-sub + pipeline forms (was bare-line only).\n207\t#\n208\t# S58 refinement (closes KI-S54-1): replaces the 3-pass grep filter with an awk script\n209\t# that tracks pipefail-bracket state + multi-line `\\`-continuation joining + 4 NEW\n210\t# false-positive recognitions surfaced by S57 ratify-via-comment categorization sample:\n211\t#\n212\t#   NEW skip rules:\n213\t#     (1) Compound conditional `if X && grep`/`if X | grep`/`elif X && grep`/etc — line\n214\t#         starts with `if|while|until|elif` AND contains `grep` (cond exit consumed by `if`)\n215\t#     (2) Alt-guard `|| echo NN`/`|| echo \"\"`/`|| echo` end-of-pipeline (substitute\n216\t#         non-zero with echo output → command-sub/pipeline returns 0 → ERR trap exempt)\n217\t#     (3) Alt-guard `|| exit N`/`|| return N` (explicit exit handling)\n218\t#     (4) Pipefail-bracket `set +o pipefail; grep; set -o pipefail` (pipefail temporarily\n219\t#         disabled so grep no-match is safe; double-defense pattern)\n220\t#\n221\t# Plus existing skip rules (preserved):\n222\t#   - Comment lines (`^NN:#`)\n223\t#   - Direct conditional `if grep`/`while grep`/`until grep`/`elif grep`\n224\t#   - Already-guarded `|| true`/`|| :`\n225\t#\n226\t# Multi-line `\\`-continuation: physical lines ending with `\\` are joined with the next\n227\t# physical line before pattern testing (catches drift-signals-D1-D9.sh:178-180 form\n228\t# where alt-guard `|| echo 0` is on the LAST continuation line of a 3-line pipeline).\n229\t#\n230\t# Initial pipefail state: OFF (matches bash default; flips ON when first\n231\t# `set -[a-zA-Z]*o pipefail` seen; flips OFF when `set +o pipefail` seen).\n232\tif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n233\t  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n234\t    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n235\t    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n236\t    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n237\t    # Clean 0/1 (per L-S80-2: avoids \"0\\n0\" multi-line from `grep -c ... || echo 0` race that breaks awk -v / [ test downstream)\n238\t    if grep -qE 'set [^#]*pipefail' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then HAS_PIPEFAIL=1; else HAS_PIPEFAIL=0; fi\n239\t    if grep -qE 'trap[[:space:]]+[^#]*ERR' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then HAS_ERR_TRAP=1; else HAS_ERR_TRAP=0; fi\n240\t    if [ \"$HAS_PIPEFAIL\" = 1 ] && [ \"$HAS_ERR_TRAP\" = 1 ]; then\n241\t      VIOLATION_LINE=\"$(awk '\n242\tBEGIN { pipefail_off = 1; carry = \"\" }\n243\t{\n244\t  line = $0\n245\t  # Match physical line ending with literal `\\` (backslash continuation).\n246\t  # /\\\\$/ in awk source = regex `\\\\` (literal backslash) + `$` (EOL anchor).\n247\t  # Confirmed empirically: `awk '/\\\\$/'` matches lines ending with literal `\\`.\n248\t  if (line ~ /\\\\$/) {\n249\t    sub(/\\\\$/, \"\", line)\n250\t    carry = (carry == \"\" ? line : carry \" \" line)\n251\t    next\n252\t  }\n253\t  full = (carry == \"\" ? line : carry \" \" line)\n254\t  carry = \"\"\n255\t\n256\t  if (full ~ /^[[:space:]]*set[[:space:]]+\\+o[[:space:]]+pipefail/) {\n257\t    pipefail_off = 1\n258\t    next\n259\t  }\n260\t  if (full ~ /^[[:space:]]*set[[:space:]]+-[a-zA-Z]*o[[:space:]]+pipefail/) {\n261\t    pipefail_off = 0\n262\t    next\n263\t  }\n264\t  if (pipefail_off) next\n265\t  if (full ~ /^[[:space:]]*#/) next\n266\t  if (full ~ /grep[[:space:]]/) {\n267\t    if (full ~ /^[[:space:]]*(if|while|until|elif)[[:space:]]/) next\n268\t    if (full ~ /grep[[:space:]].*[[:space:]](&&|\\|\\|)[[:space:]]/) next\n269\t    print NR \":\" full\n270\t    exit\n271\t  }\n272\t}\n273\t' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n274\t      if [ -n \"$VIOLATION_LINE\" ]; then\n275\t        emit \"L-S48d-1-PIPEFAIL-BARE-GREP\" \"$bn\" \"pipefail + ERR trap + grep without guard — silent fail risk (L-S48d-1; refined S58 to recognize compound-if + alt-guard echo/exit/return + pipefail-bracket + multi-line continuation)\"\n276\t      fi\n277\t    fi\n278\t  done\n279\tfi\n280\t\n281\t# === Check 8: L-S53-2 unanchored positional-marker grep (NEW Pattern D) — S54 ===\n282\t# Origin: S53 M-S53-2 — `session-export-raw.sh` Method 1 grep `'S[0-9]+[[:space:]]+NEXT'`\n283\t# without ^ anchor matched archive prose mid-line \"S38/S42 NEXT branching gate\" →\n284\t# returned false-positive 42 every export, silently corrupted ALL raw-session filenames.\n285\t# Detection: 2-pass — (a) find grep lines whose pattern contains routing-marker regex\n286\t# tokens (S[0-9]+, Track [0-9]+, Session N, Session [0-9]+, ## S[0-9]+); (b) exclude\n287\t# lines where the pattern body starts with `^` anchor (whitelist).\n288\tif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n289\t  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n290\t    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n291\t    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n292\t    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n293\t    BAD_D=\"$(grep -nE \"grep[[:space:]]+(-[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9]*[[:space:]]+)*['\\\"][^'\\\"]*(S\\[0-9\\]\\+|Track[[:space:]]+\\[0-9|Session[[:space:]]+(N|\\[0-9)|## S\\[0-9)\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null \\\n294\t      | grep -vE \"grep[[:space:]]+(-[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9]*[[:space:]]+)*['\\\"]\\^\" \\\n295\t      | grep -vE '^[[:space:]]*[0-9]+:[[:space:]]*#' \\\n296\t      || true)\"\n297\t    if [ -n \"$BAD_D\" ]; then\n298\t      emit \"L-S53-2-UNANCHORED-POSITIONAL-GREP\" \"$bn\" \"grep with routing-marker pattern (S<N>/Track/Session/## S<N>) lacks '^' anchor — mid-line false-positive risk (L-S53-2)\"\n299\t    fi\n300\t  done\n301\tfi\n302\t\n303\t# === Check 9: L-S68-2 family — find/ls on possibly-missing path under pipefail+ERR-trap (NEW S80) ===\n304\t# Origin: 4 instances across S68/S75/S76/S78. Family precondition: file has BOTH `set -*o pipefail`\n305\t# AND `trap ... ERR`. In that mode, `find $missing -mtime`/`ls $dir/*.glob` returns rc=1 silently\n306\t# if path missing or glob has no match → ERR trap fires → script silent-exits 0 BEFORE work runs.\n307\t#\n308\t# Variants (combined detector):\n309\t#   (a) Single-path `find $path -...` (literal var; no glob) — S68+S75\n310\t#   (b) `ls $dir/*.glob` (no-match returns rc=1 under pipefail) — S76\n311\t#   (c) Multi-path `find $a $b $c -...` (rc=1 if ANY arg missing) — S78\n312\t#\n313\t# Skip rules (all three variants):\n314\t#   - Comment lines (^NN: # )\n315\t#   - Line starts with `if|while|until|elif`\n316\t#   - Alt-guarded with `|| true|:|return|exit|echo`\n317\t#   - Errors silenced with `2>/dev/null`\n318\t#   - Pipefail bracket disabled (`set +o pipefail` in effect)\n319\t#   - File-test guard `[ -d ... ]` or `[ -f ... ]` on SAME line (catches `[ -d $X ] && find $X`)\n320\t#   - Variant (b) only: `shopt -s nullglob` set anywhere above (glob no-match → empty list, no rc=1)\n321\t#\n322\t# Multi-line `\\`-continuation: physical lines joined with carry before pattern-matching, so multi-arg\n323\t# find spread across multiple lines is tested as one logical line (catches `2>/dev/null` on tail).\n324\tif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n325\t  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n326\t    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n327\t    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n328\t    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n329\t    # Clean 0/1 (per L-S80-2: avoids \"0\\n0\" multi-line from `grep -c ... || echo 0` race that breaks awk -v / [ test downstream)\n330\t    if grep -qE 'set [^#]*pipefail' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then HAS_PIPEFAIL=1; else HAS_PIPEFAIL=0; fi\n331\t    if grep -qE 'trap[[:space:]]+[^#]*ERR' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then HAS_ERR_TRAP=1; else HAS_ERR_TRAP=0; fi\n332\t    if [ \"$HAS_PIPEFAIL\" = 1 ] && [ \"$HAS_ERR_TRAP\" = 1 ]; then\n333\t      # Clean 0/1 (avoids \"0\\n0\" from `grep -c` exit-1 + `|| echo 0` race that breaks awk's numeric coercion)\n334\t      if grep -qE '^[[:space:]]*shopt[[:space:]]+-s[[:space:]]+nullglob' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then HAS_NULLGLOB=1; else HAS_NULLGLOB=0; fi\n335\t      VIOLATION_LINE_E=\"$(awk -v has_nullglob=\"$HAS_NULLGLOB\" '\n336\tBEGIN { pipefail_off = 1; carry = \"\" }\n337\t{\n338\t  line = $0\n339\t  # Match physical line ending with literal `\\` (backslash continuation).\n340\t  # /\\\\$/ in awk source = regex `\\\\` (literal backslash) + `$` (EOL anchor).\n341\t  # Confirmed empirically: `awk '/\\\\$/'` matches lines ending with literal `\\`.\n342\t  if (line ~ /\\\\$/) {\n343\t    sub(/\\\\$/, \"\", line)\n344\t    carry = (carry == \"\" ? line : carry \" \" line)\n345\t    next\n346\t  }\n347\t  full = (carry == \"\" ? line : carry \" \" line)\n348\t  carry = \"\"\n349\t\n350\t  if (full ~ /^[[:space:]]*set[[:space:]]+\\+o[[:space:]]+pipefail/) { pipefail_off = 1; next }\n351\t  if (full ~ /^[[:space:]]*set[[:space:]]+-[a-zA-Z]*o[[:space:]]+pipefail/) { pipefail_off = 0; next }\n352\t  if (pipefail_off) next\n353\t  if (full ~ /^[[:space:]]*#/) next\n354\t\n355\t  body = full\n356\t  sub(/^[[:space:]]+/, \"\", body)\n357\t\n358\t  # Skip lines starting with conditional keywords\n359\t  if (body ~ /^(if|while|until|elif)[[:space:]]/) next\n360\t\n361\t  # Skip alt-guarded with || true|:|return|exit|echo\n362\t  if (full ~ /\\|\\|[[:space:]]+(true|:|return|exit|echo)/) next\n363\t\n364\t  # Skip if errors silenced\n365\t  if (full ~ /2>\\/dev\\/null/) next\n366\t\n367\t  # Skip if same-line file-test guard (catches `[ -d $X ] && find $X`)\n368\t  if (full ~ /\\[[[:space:]]+-[df][[:space:]]+/) next\n369\t\n370\t  # === Variant (a) + (c): find <var/literal>... ===\n371\t  # Match `find` token followed by quoted/unquoted $var arg.\n372\t  if (body ~ /(^|[[:space:]`(|&;])find[[:space:]]+[\"]?\\$/) {\n373\t    print NR \":(find-variant) \" full\n374\t    exit\n375\t  }\n376\t\n377\t  # === Variant (b): ls $dir/*.glob ===\n378\t  # Match `ls` token followed by arg(s) containing a `$var` AND a glob `*` somewhere after.\n379\t  # Permissive on intermediate chars (handles `ls \"$DIR\"/*.md`, `ls ${X}/*.tsv`, etc.).\n380\t  # Skip if shopt -s nullglob set in file (glob no-match → empty list, no rc=1).\n381\t  if (has_nullglob == 0 && body ~ /(^|[[:space:]`(|&;])ls[[:space:]]+[^|&;]*\\$[^|&;]*\\*/) {\n382\t    print NR \":(ls-glob-variant) \" full\n383\t    exit\n384\t  }\n385\t}\n386\t' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n387\t      if [ -n \"$VIOLATION_LINE_E\" ]; then\n388\t        emit \"L-S68-2-FIND-LS-MISSING-PATH\" \"$bn\" \"find/ls on possibly-missing path under pipefail+ERR-trap — silent exit risk if path missing (L-S68-2 family; 4 instances S68/S75/S76/S78). Use [ -d \\$X ]/[ -f \\$X ] guard, OR add 2>/dev/null + || true, OR conditional form.\"\n389\t      fi\n390\t    fi\n391\t  done\n392\tfi\n393\t\n394\t# === Check 10: L-S80-2 grep-c-OR-echo capture trap (Pattern F, NEW S81) ===\n395\t# Origin: S80 T2 debug — bash-hook-lint.sh HAS_NULLGLOB extracted via\n396\t#   `HAS_NULLGLOB=\"$(grep -c ... 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"` produces multi-line \"0\\n0\" when grep\n397\t# finds 0 + exits 1 (because grep -c always prints \"0\" THEN || fires → echo prints another \"0\"\n398\t# → captured stdout is \"0\\n0\"). Passing to awk `-v var=...` then `var == 0` evaluates as\n399\t# string \"0\\n0\" != numeric 0 → false-negative; nullglob detection skipped → ls-glob detector\n400\t# fires false-positive. Recovered S80 via if/then/fi clean-integer fix.\n401\t#\n402\t# Detection: VAR=$(... grep -c ... || echo <N> ...) with || INSIDE the $() (capture trap).\n403\t# The form is intrinsic to grep -c semantics + || short-circuit, regardless of pipefail/ERR-trap\n404\t# state — so no precondition check (unlike Check 7/9 family).\n405\t#\n406\t# Skip rules:\n407\t#   - Comment lines (^NN:#)\n408\t# NOTE: intermediate flattener pipes (`| head`, `| tr -d`, `| awk`) do NOT reliably mitigate\n409\t# under `set -o pipefail` — pipeline still propagates grep's non-zero exit → || still fires →\n410\t# echo appends its own newline → multi-line capture survives. L-S10-1: if/then/fi only; split-local.\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nHOOKS_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\nVIOLATIONS=0\nVIOLATION_LIST=\"\"\n\nemit() {\n  local code=\"$1\" file=\"$2\" detail=\"$3\"\n  VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n  VIOLATION_LIST=\"${VIOLATION_LIST}  - ${code}: ${file} — ${detail}\"$'\\n'\n}\n\n# === Check 1: L-S11-1 Phase 0 portability — no python/jq/yq/pip/npm in scripts/hooks ===\n# Exempt: comments, strings, and explicit Phase-1+ stubs marked with \"# PHASE-1+\" guard comment.\nif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n    # Skip self.\n    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n    # Strip comments + heredocs roughly: scan only \"executable\" lines (not # comments).\n    NON_COMMENT=\"$(grep -vE '^[[:space:]]*#' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n    # Look for forbidden tooling invocations as command tokens.\n    if printf '%s' \"$NON_COMMENT\" | grep -qE '(^|[[:space:]`(|&;])(python3?|jq|yq|pip|pip3|npm|pnpm)([[:space:]]|$)'; then\n      emit \"L-S11-1-PORTABILITY\" \"$bn\" \"non-Phase-0-portable invocation (python/jq/yq/pip/npm) — Phase 0 doctrine = bash + POSIX only\"\n    fi\n  done\nfi\n\n# === Check 4: L-S43b-9 printf format-string starting with `-` needs `--` sentinel ===\n# `printf '-foo'` interprets `-foo` as an option flag, not as the format string. L-S48m-1 fix: use session-log basename instead.\nif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n    BAD_B=\"$(grep -nE \"[\\\"]?\\\\.[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+(-fired|-marker|-ran|-flag)-\\\\\\$\\\\{?CLAUDE_SESSION_ID\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -vE \"^[[:space:]]*[0-9]+:[[:space:]]*#\" || true)\"\n    if [ -n \"$BAD_B\" ]; then\n      emit \"L-S48m-1-CLAUDE-SESSION-ID-MARKER\" \"$bn\" \"marker filename uses \\$CLAUDE_SESSION_ID (empty on Windows; silent skip — L-S48m-1)\"\n    fi\n  done\nfi\n\n# === Check 6b: L-S108-1 CLAUDE_SESSION_ID fallback-to-constant anti-pattern (Pattern B') ===\n# Origin: S108 qa-pending-auto-mover.sh — SID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-main}\" then\n# MARKER=\"...-${SID}\". then\n      VIOLATION_LINE=\"$(awk '\nBEGIN { pipefail_off = 1; carry = \"\" }\n{\n  line = $0\n  # Match physical line ending with literal `\\` (backslash continuation).\n  # /\\\\$/ in awk source = regex `\\\\` (literal backslash) + `$` (EOL anchor).\n  # Confirmed empirically: `awk '/\\\\$/'` matches lines ending with literal `\\`.\n  if (line ~ /\\\\$/) {\n    sub(/\\\\$/, \"\", line)\n    carry = (carry == \"\" ? line : carry \" \" line)\n    next\n  }\n  full = (carry == \"\" ? line : carry \" \" line)\n  carry = \"\"\n\n  if (full ~ /^[[:space:]]*set[[:space:]]+\\+o[[:space:]]+pipefail/) {\n    pipefail_off = 1\n    next\n  }\n  if (full ~ /^[[:space:]]*set[[:space:]]+-[a-zA-Z]*o[[:space:]]+pipefail/) {\n    pipefail_off = 0\n    next\n  }\n  if (pipefail_off) next\n  if (full ~ /^[[:space:]]*#/) next\n  if (full ~ /grep[[:space:]]/) {\n    if (full ~ /^[[:space:]]*(if|while|until|elif)[[:space:]]/) next\n    if (full ~ /grep[[:space:]].*[[:space:]](&&|\\|\\|)[[:space:]]/) next\n    print NR \":\" full\n    exit\n  }\n}\n' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n      if [ -n \"$VIOLATION_LINE\" ]; then\n        emit \"L-S48d-1-PIPEFAIL-BARE-GREP\" \"$bn\" \"pipefail + ERR trap + grep without guard — silent fail risk (L-S48d-1; refined S58 to recognize compound-if + alt-guard echo/exit/return + pipefail-bracket + multi-line continuation)\"\n      fi\n    fi\n  done\nfi\n\n# === Check 8: L-S53-2 unanchored positional-marker grep (NEW Pattern D) — S54 ===\n# Origin: S53 M-S53-2 — `session-export-raw.sh` Method 1 grep `'S[0-9]+[[:space:]]+NEXT'`\n# without ^ anchor matched archive prose mid-line \"S38/S42 NEXT branching gate\" →\n# returned false-positive 42 every export, silently corrupted ALL raw-session filenames.\n# Detection: 2-pass — (a) find grep lines whose pattern contains routing-marker regex\n# tokens (S[0-9]+, Track [0-9]+, Session N, Session [0-9]+, ## S[0-9]+); (b) exclude\n# lines where the pattern body starts with `^` anchor (whitelist).\nif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n    BAD_D=\"$(grep -nE \"grep[[:space:]]+(-[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9]*[[:space:]]+)*['\\\"][^'\\\"]*(S\\[0-9\\]\\+|Track[[:space:]]+\\[0-9|Session[[:space:]]+(N|\\[0-9)|## S\\[0-9)\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null \\\n      | grep -vE \"grep[[:space:]]+(-[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9]*[[:space:]]+)*['\\\"]\\^\" \\\n      | grep -vE '^[[:space:]]*[0-9]+:[[:space:]]*#' \\\n      || true)\"\n    if [ -n \"$BAD_D\" ]; then\n      emit \"L-S53-2-UNANCHORED-POSITIONAL-GREP\" \"$bn\" \"grep with routing-marker pattern (S<N>/Track/Session/## S<N>) lacks '^' anchor — mid-line false-positive risk (L-S53-2)\"\n    fi\n  done\nfi\n\n# === Check 9: L-S68-2 family — find/ls on possibly-missing path under pipefail+ERR-trap (NEW S80) ===\n# Origin: 4 instances across S68/S75/S76/S78. Family precondition: file has BOTH `set -*o pipefail`\n# AND `trap ... ERR`. In that mode, `find $missing -mtime`/`ls $dir/*.glob` returns rc=1 silently\n# if path missing or glob has no match → ERR trap fires → script silent-exits 0 BEFORE work runs.\n#\n# Variants (combined detector):\n#   (a) Single-path `find $path -...` (literal var; no glob) — S68+S75\n#   (b) `ls $dir/*.glob` (no-match returns rc=1 under pipefail) — S76\n#   (c) Multi-path `find $a $b $c -...` (rc=1 if ANY arg missing) — S78\n#\n# Skip rules (all three variants):\n#   - Comment lines (^NN: # )\n#   - Line starts with `if|while|until|elif`\n#   - Alt-guarded with `|| true|:|return|exit|echo`\n#   - Errors silenced with `2>/dev/null`\n#   - Pipefail bracket disabled (`set +o pipefail` in effect)\n#   - File-test guard `[ -d ... ]` or `[ -f ... ]` on SAME line (catches `[ -d $X ] && find $X`)\n#   - Variant (b) only: `shopt -s nullglob` set anywhere above (glob no-match → empty list, no rc=1)\n#\n# Multi-line `\\`-continuation: physical lines joined with carry before pattern-matching, so multi-arg\n# find spread across multiple lines is tested as one logical line (catches `2>/dev/null` on tail).\nif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n    # Clean 0/1 (per L-S80-2: avoids \"0\\n0\" multi-line from `grep -c ... || echo 0` race that breaks awk -v / [ test downstream)\n    if grep -qE 'set [^#]*pipefail' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then HAS_PIPEFAIL=1; else HAS_PIPEFAIL=0; fi\n    if grep -qE 'trap[[:space:]]+[^#]*ERR' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then HAS_ERR_TRAP=1; else HAS_ERR_TRAP=0; fi\n    if [ \"$HAS_PIPEFAIL\" = 1 ] && [ \"$HAS_ERR_TRAP\" = 1 ]; then\n      # Clean 0/1 (avoids \"0\\n0\" from `grep -c` exit-1 + `|| echo 0` race that breaks awk's numeric coercion)\n      if grep -qE '^[[:space:]]*shopt[[:space:]]+-s[[:space:]]+nullglob' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then HAS_NULLGLOB=1; else HAS_NULLGLOB=0; fi\n      VIOLATION_LINE_E=\"$(awk -v has_nullglob=\"$HAS_NULLGLOB\" '\nBEGIN { pipefail_off = 1; carry = \"\" }\n{\n  line = $0\n  # Match physical line ending with literal `\\` (backslash continuation).\n  # /\\\\$/ in awk source = regex `\\\\` (literal backslash) + `$` (EOL anchor).\n  # Confirmed empirically: `awk '/\\\\$/'` matches lines ending with literal `\\`.\n  if (line ~ /\\\\$/) {\n    sub(/\\\\$/, \"\", line)\n    carry = (carry == \"\" ? line : carry \" \" line)\n    next\n  }\n  full = (carry == \"\" ? line : carry \" \" line)\n  carry = \"\"\n\n  if (full ~ /^[[:space:]]*set[[:space:]]+\\+o[[:space:]]+pipefail/) { pipefail_off = 1; next }\n  if (full ~ /^[[:space:]]*set[[:space:]]+-[a-zA-Z]*o[[:space:]]+pipefail/) { pipefail_off = 0; next }\n  if (pipefail_off) next\n  if (full ~ /^[[:space:]]*#/) next\n\n  body = full\n  sub(/^[[:space:]]+/, \"\", body)\n\n  # Skip lines starting with conditional keywords\n  if (body ~ /^(if|while|until|elif)[[:space:]]/) next\n\n  # Skip alt-guarded with || true|:|return|exit|echo\n  if (full ~ /\\|\\|[[:space:]]+(true|:|return|exit|echo)/) next\n\n  # Skip if errors silenced\n  if (full ~ /2>\\/dev\\/null/) next\n\n  # Skip if same-line file-test guard (catches `[ -d $X ] && find $X`)\n  if (full ~ /\\[[[:space:]]+-[df][[:space:]]+/) next\n\n  # === Variant (a) + (c): find <var/literal>... ===\n  # Match `find` token followed by quoted/unquoted $var arg.\n  if (body ~ /(^|[[:space:]`(|&;])find[[:space:]]+[\"]?\\$/) {\n    print NR \":(find-variant) \" full\n    exit\n  }\n\n  # === Variant (b): ls $dir/*.glob ===\n  # Match `ls` token followed by arg(s) containing a `$var` AND a glob `*` somewhere after.\n  # Permissive on intermediate chars (handles `ls \"$DIR\"/*.md`, `ls ${X}/*.tsv`, etc.).\n  # Skip if shopt -s nullglob set in file (glob no-match → empty list, no rc=1).\n  if (has_nullglob == 0 && body ~ /(^|[[:space:]`(|&;])ls[[:space:]]+[^|&;]*\\$[^|&;]*\\*/) {\n    print NR \":(ls-glob-variant) \" full\n    exit\n  }\n}\n' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n      if [ -n \"$VIOLATION_LINE_E\" ]; then\n        emit \"L-S68-2-FIND-LS-MISSING-PATH\" \"$bn\" \"find/ls on possibly-missing path under pipefail+ERR-trap — silent exit risk if path missing (L-S68-2 family; 4 instances S68/S75/S76/S78). Use [ -d \\$X ]/[ -f \\$X ] guard, OR add 2>/dev/null + || true, OR conditional form.\"\n      fi\n    fi\n  done\nfi\n\n# === Check 10: L-S80-2 grep-c-OR-echo capture trap (Pattern F, NEW S81) ===\n# Origin: S80 T2 debug — bash-hook-lint.sh HAS_NULLGLOB extracted via\n#   `HAS_NULLGLOB=\"$(grep -c ... 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"` produces multi-line \"0\\n0\" when grep\n# finds 0 + exits 1 (because grep -c always prints \"0\" THEN || fires → echo prints another \"0\"\n# → captured stdout is \"0\\n0\"). Passing to awk `-v var=...` then `var == 0` evaluates as\n# string \"0\\n0\" != numeric 0 → false-negative; nullglob detection skipped → ls-glob detector\n# fires false-positive. Recovered S80 via if/then/fi clean-integer fix.\n#\n# Detection: VAR=$(... grep -c ... || echo <N> ...) with || INSIDE the $() (capture trap).\n# The form is intrinsic to grep -c semantics + || short-circuit, regardless of pipefail/ERR-trap\n# state — so no precondition check (unlike Check 7/9 family).\n#\n# Skip rules:\n#   - Comment lines (^NN:#)\n# NOTE: intermediate flattener pipes (`| head`, `| tr -d`, `| awk`) do NOT reliably mitigate\n# under `set -o pipefail` — pipeline still propagates grep's non-zero exit → || still fires →\n# echo appends its own newline → multi-line capture survives. Always-flag; only fix is if/then/fi.\nif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n    VIOLATION_LINE_F=\"$(awk '\n/^[[:space:]]*#/ { next }\n{\n  # Pattern F: =$( ... grep ... -c ... || echo <N> ... )  with || INSIDE the $()\n  # `[^)]*` between `$(` and `)` rejects nested `)` (rare in practice; acceptable FN).\n  # No flattener-pipe skip rule: under `set -o pipefail` the pipeline returns non-zero\n  # when grep returns 1, so `|| echo N` fires regardless of intermediate `head`/`tr -d`/`awk`,\n  # and echo appends its own newline → multi-line capture survives the flattener.\n  # Only safe mitigation = if/then/fi clean-integer form.\n  if ($0 !~ /=[[:space:]]*\"?\\$\\([^)]*grep[^)]*[[:space:]]-c[A-Za-z]*[[:space:]][^)]*\\|\\|[[:space:]]*echo[[:space:]]+[0-9]/) next\n  print NR \":\" $0\n  exit\n}\n' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n    if [ -n \"$VIOLATION_LINE_F\" ]; then\n      emit \"L-S80-2-GREP-C-OR-ECHO-CAPTURE-TRAP\" \"$bn\" \"VAR=\\$(grep -c ... || echo N) produces multi-line \\\"0\\\\nN\\\" capture when grep finds 0 + exits 1 → breaks awk -v numeric coercion downstream (L-S80-2). Fix: if grep -qE ...; then VAR=1; else VAR=0; fi (clean integer).\"\n    fi\n  done\nfi\n\n# === Emit results ===\nif [ \"$VIOLATIONS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  printf '[%s] bash-hook-lint WARN %d violation(s):\\n%s' \"$TS\" \"$VIOLATIONS\" \"$VIOLATION_LIST\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  if [ -d \"$NOTIF_DIR\" ]; then\n    TS_FILE=\"$(date -u +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)\"\n    NOTIF_FILE=\"$NOTIF_DIR/$TS_FILE-bash-hook-lint-warn.md\"\n    {\n      printf '# bash-hook-lint — Warnings\\n\\n'\n      printf 'Per L-S11-1 + L-S13-1 + S16 D-IDENTITY (S15 close user correction): %d violation(s) detected.\\n\\n' \"$VIOLATIONS\"\n      printf '%s' \"$VIOLATION_LIST\"\n      printf '\\nFix:\\n'\n      printf '- L-S11-1: replace python/jq/yq/pip/npm with bash + POSIX equivalents (Phase 0 portability)\\n'\n      printf '- L-S13-1: ensure declared LOG/FILE/OUTPUT vars get used as `>> \"$VAR\"` or read source\\n'\n      printf '- D-IDENTITY: live config must NOT reference SUPERVISED mode / autonomous_mode=false / until-Track-7 framing\\n'\n      printf -- '- L-S43b-9: replace `printf \"-...\"` or `printf \"\\047-...\"\\047` with `printf -- \"-...\"` to disambiguate format from option flag\\n'\n      printf '- M-S51-1 (Pattern A): replace literal `Session N` placeholder in grep/sed/awk with real `## S[0-9]+` header pattern (verify against current-execution.md format)\\n'\n      printf '- L-S48m-1 (Pattern B): use session-log basename (NOT $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID empty-on-Windows) for marker filenames\\n'\n      printf '- L-S48d-1 (Pattern C): wrap grep usages with `|| true` (or `|| :`); use `if grep ...; then` form for conditionals; applies to bare-line + pipeline + command-substitution `$(grep ...)` forms (S54 refined)\\n'\n      printf '- L-S53-2 (Pattern D, NEW S54): anchor positional-marker grep patterns with `^` (e.g. `grep -E \"^S[0-9]+ NEXT\"` not `grep -E \"S[0-9]+ NEXT\"`) to prevent mid-line archive-prose false-positives\\n'\n      printf '- L-S68-2 (Pattern E, NEW S80): wrap `find $var ...` / `ls $dir/*.glob` with `2>/dev/null` + `|| true`, OR add `[ -d \"$X\" ] && find ...` same-line guard, OR `if find ...; then` conditional form. Three variants caught: (a) single-path find (S68+S75); (b) ls glob no-match (S76); (c) multi-path find (S78). Family precondition: file has both `set -*o pipefail` AND `trap ... ERR`.\\n'\n      printf '- L-S80-2 (Pattern F, NEW S81): replace `VAR=$(grep -c ... || echo N)` with `if grep -qE ...; then VAR=1; else VAR=0; fi` (clean integer). The antipattern produces multi-line \"0\\\\nN\" when grep finds 0 + exits 1 (grep -c always prints count THEN || fires → echo appends N) → breaks awk -v numeric coercion (`var == 0` evaluates as string mismatch) and `[ \"$VAR\" = 0 ]`/`-eq 0` becomes fragile. Under `set -o pipefail`, intermediate `| head`/`| tr -d`/`| awk` does NOT fully mitigate (pipeline still returns non-zero → || still fires → echo appends newline) — only if/then/fi clean-integer form is safe.\\n'\n    } > \"$NOTIF_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  fi\nelse\n  printf '[%s] bash-hook-lint OK (0 violations)\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$LOG\"\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","numLines":464,"startLine":1,"totalLines":464}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"85a740ea-7804-4350-9bcc-e6e4a287c36c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"22a3281c-5649-489a-a4fd-0f2f1ab6d011","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"2799a1a0-3e4e-4ae2-98ca-3f38832f3f7f","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01ACHCRRH4GHF9KtTK8tpupz","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01CGxk3J69SRP4TjpNiyS7NV","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\harness-health-self-scan.sh","old_string":"# === HH-1: Stop hook fires ≥1/session (KI-S49b-1 suppress: HIGH→MEDIUM) ===\nHH1_check() {\n  [ ! -f \"$HOOK_LOG\" ] && { emit_skip \"HH-1\" \"log-missing\"; return; }\n  local start_line stop_count\n  start_line=$(grep -n \"SessionStart session=$SID\" \"$HOOK_LOG\" 2>/dev/null | tail -1 | cut -d: -f1)\n  [ -z \"$start_line\" ] && { emit_skip \"HH-1\" \"no-SessionStart-for-SID\"; return; }\n  stop_count=$(awk \"NR>$start_line\" \"$HOOK_LOG\" 2>/dev/null | grep -c \"Stop session=$SID\" || echo 0)\n  [[ \"$stop_count\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || stop_count=0\n  if [ \"$stop_count\" -lt 1 ]; then\n    # KI-S49b-1 suppression check\n    local sev=\"HIGH\"\n    [ -f \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.harness-health-ki-S49b-1\" ] && sev=\"MEDIUM\"\n    emit_fail \"HH-1-STOP-NOT-FIRING\" \"$sev\" \"stop_count=0 (Windows quirk M-S49b-1)\"\n  fi\n}","new_string":"# === HH-1: Stop hook fires ≥1/session (KI-S49b-1 suppress: HIGH→MEDIUM) ===\n# S201 fix: when CLAUDE_SESSION_ID is empty (Windows quirk → SID=\"unknown\"), the\n# `SessionStart session=$SID` literal match returned 0 → emit_skip every run →\n# HH-1 became dormant. Fall back to matching the latest SessionStart line in\n# the log (regardless of SID), which is correct in single-active-session contexts.\nHH1_check() {\n  [ ! -f \"$HOOK_LOG\" ] && { emit_skip \"HH-1\" \"log-missing\"; return; }\n  local start_line stop_count match_pattern\n  if [ \"$SID\" = \"unknown\" ] || [ -z \"$SID\" ]; then\n    match_pattern=\"SessionStart session=\"\n  else\n    match_pattern=\"SessionStart session=$SID\"\n  fi\n  start_line=$(grep -n \"$match_pattern\" \"$HOOK_LOG\" 2>/dev/null | tail -1 | cut -d: -f1)\n  [ -z \"$start_line\" ] && { emit_skip \"HH-1\" \"no-SessionStart-found\"; return; }\n  if [ \"$SID\" = \"unknown\" ] || [ -z \"$SID\" ]; then\n    if awk \"NR>$start_line\" \"$HOOK_LOG\" 2>/dev/null | grep -qE \"Stop session=\"; then\n      stop_count=1\n    else\n      stop_count=0\n    fi\n  else\n    if awk \"NR>$start_line\" \"$HOOK_LOG\" 2>/dev/null | grep -qE \"Stop session=$SID\"; Result: 2 of the 3 \"bug classes\" S200 identified turn out to be misdiagnoses based on wrong detection signals; the actual bash-hook-lint Guardian itself has a false-positive regex bug (Check 6b) that flagged the L-S108-1 FIX as if it were the L-S108-1 BUG. Meta-lesson: even Guardians need calibration; \"Guardian-first\" doctrine still requires verifying the Guardian's claim against the ground-truth code.\n\n## Re-classification of S200's \"3 bug classes\"\n\n### Bug Class 1 (effort-escalation MIS-WIRED) — RETIRED (misdiagnosis)\n\n**S200 framing**: `effort-escalation-detector.sh:34` guard `HOOK_EVENT=PreToolUse && TOOL_NAME=Agent` → MIS-WIRED to UserPromptSubmit → always early-exits.\n\n**S201 verification (read source verbatim)**:\n- Line 33-34: `[ \"${HOOK_EVENT:-}\" = \"UserPromptSubmit\" ] || { [ \"${HOOK_EVENT:-}\" = \"PreToolUse\" ] && [ \"${TOOL_NAME:-}\" = \"Agent\" ]; } || exit 0`\n- Guard reads as: pass IF `HOOK_EVENT=UserPromptSubmit` OR `(HOOK_EVENT=PreToolUse AND TOOL_NAME=Agent)`. Hook IS designed for both events.\n- Settings.json line 458: hook ALSO already registered as `PreToolUse` matcher `.*`. So both event paths covered.\n- Hook never writes to `.effort-escalation.log` (only stderr at line 108). The \"log MISSING\" detection signal in S187..S200 observations was WRONG — the file is never created by design.\n\n**Verdict**: NO BUG. S200's \"log MISSING = silent\" detection signal was invalid. No fix needed.\n\n### Bug Class 2 (idle-escape + phase-coherence L-S108-1 lockout) — RETIRED (misdiagnosis)\n\n**S200 framing**: bash-hook-lint flags `${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-WORD}` fallback-to-constant + marker filename per-session lockout.\n\n**S201 verification**:\n- `idle-escape-detector.sh` lines 36-38: `BUCKET=\"$(date +%Y%m%d-%H 2>/dev/null)\"; [ -z \"$BUCKET\" ] && BUCKET=\"unbucketed-$$\"; MARKER=\"...-fired-${BUCKET}\"` — date-hour-bucket fix ALREADY PRESENT (per S108 prevention rule).\n- `phase-status-coherence.sh` lines 39-41: identical pattern; date-hour-bucket fix ALREADY PRESENT.\n- `project-md-adr-staleness.sh` lines 37-39: identical pattern; date-hour-bucket fix ALREADY PRESENT.\n- All 3 hooks ALREADY apply L-S108-1 prevention correctly.\n\n**Real bug found**: `bash-hook-lint.sh` Check 6b at line 192 has overly broad regex `\\.[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+-(fired|marker|ran|flag|written|pending)-\\$\\{?[A-Z_]` which matches `${BUCKET}` (and any `[A-Z_]`-prefixed variable) — flagging the L-S108-1 SAFE pattern as if it were the BUG. This is a Guardian false-positive that misled S200 (and S187..S199 by extension since this lint output had been emitting since S195).\n\n**Fix applied (S201 IMPL)**: Edit `bash-hook-lint.sh` Check 6b to whitelist `BUCKET`-token marker filenames:\n```bash\nHAS_MARKER=\"$(grep -nE '\\.[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+-(fired|...)-\\$\\{?[A-Z_]' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null \\\n              | grep -vE \"^[[:space:]]*[0-9]+:[[:space:]]*#\" \\\n              | grep -vE '\\$\\{?[A-Z_]*BUCKET[A-Z_]*\\}?' \\    # NEW S201\n              || true)\"\n```\n\n**Post-fix verification**:\n- bash -n OK\n- bash-hook-lint run on full hooks/ tree: 23 violations → **20 violations** (3 L-S108-1 false positives eliminated for idle-escape, phase-coherence, project-md-adr-staleness).\n- Notification file `human-workspace/notifications/20260509-035209-bash-hook-lint-warn.md` confirmed: ZERO L-S108-1 entries (only the static fix-suggestion footer mentions L-S108-1).\n\n### Bug Class 3 (harness-health SID=\"unknown\" via fallback) → `start_line` empty → `emit_skip \"HH-1\" \"no-SessionStart-for-SID\"` always fires → HH-1 dormant.\n\n**S201 verification**:\n- `.session-hooks.log` confirms `[2026-05-09T10:47:17+07:00] SessionStart session=` (empty SID literal) in real prod.\n- HH-1 has been silent-skipping every run since CLAUDE_SESSION_ID env became empty on Windows.\n\n**Fix applied (S201 IMPL)**: Edit `harness-health-self-scan.sh` HH1_check to handle empty/unknown SID by matching the latest `SessionStart session=` line regardless of SID, and matching `Stop session=` similarly. ~16 LOC change.\n\n**Post-fix verification**:\n- bash -n OK\n- Firing-test `harness-health-self-scan-fire-test.sh`: **7/7 PASS** (smoke + HH-7 stale + HH-11 frozen + GREEN + cache-hit-skip).\n\n## {"parentUuid":"0c3793c0-009c-429d-acd5-c0b92d79edc3","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"33358c32-1f40-4665-adc4-dc1cd1282a97","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01RhujY4J4kkPqtUEaYF4xwh","type":"tool_result","content":"The file C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\idle-escape-detector.sh has been updated successfully."}]},"uuid":"f71a5ed8-a8da-4f56-8a90-aaeef6a1c6e9","timestamp":"2026-05-09T04:00:28.717Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\idle-escape-detector.sh","oldString":"HEADERS=$(grep -m 5 -E '^## S[0-9]+[a-z]?[[:space:]]+—[[:space:]]+Phase' \"$CE\" 2>/dev/null)\nHEADER_COUNT=$(echo \"$HEADERS\" | grep -c '^## S' 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n[[ \"$HEADER_COUNT\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || HEADER_COUNT=0","newString":"HEADERS=$(grep -m 5 -E '^## S[0-9]+[a-z]?[[:space:]]+—[[:space:]]+Phase' \"$CE\" 2>/dev/null)\n# L-S80-2 fix: clean integer 1/0 (downstream only checks -lt 1).\nif echo \"$HEADERS\" | grep -q '^## S' 2>/dev/null; then HEADER_COUNT=1; else HEADER_COUNT=0; fi","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# idle-escape-detector.sh — UserPromptSubmit + SessionStart hook.\n# Detects ≥3 consecutive ROUTINE-IDLE / META-CADENCE session blocks at the top\n# (newest) of agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md and demands an escape\n# action (phase-audit subagent dispatch OR Q&A escalation) before agent slips\n# into a 4th idle session.\n#\n# Phase 3.5 / S175 deliverable. M-S171-1 prevention rule #3.\n# Pairs with phase-status-coherence.sh (same session; complementary check).\n#\n# Source-of-truth pattern markers (anchored from S148-S170 production blocks):\n#   ## S<N> — Phase <X> — ROUTINE-IDLE: ...\n#   ## S<N> — Phase <X> — META-CADENCE: ...\n# Both are zero-substantive-work session classes. ≥3 consecutive at top = the\n# 22-session stall pattern that surfaced as M-S171-1 (S148-S170, ~100K wasted main).\n#\n# Per CLAUDE.md hard rule + Phase 3.5 Hard Rule #2: every hook ships with companion\n# firing-test (scripts/hooks/firing-tests/idle-escape-detector-fire-test.sh).\n# Per UP-05 + L-S11-1: bash + POSIX tools only; no Skill tool calls.\n# Exit 0 ALWAYS (informational, not blocking).\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nSID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-unknown}\"\nEVENT=\"${CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT:-UserPromptSubmit}\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\nCE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\"\nPLANS_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/session-plans/pending\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nIDLE_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.idle-escape.log\"\n\n# Per-hour-bucket idempotency marker per L-S108-1 (CLAUDE_SESSION_ID may be empty\n# under SessionStart on Windows → fallback to constant collides across sessions).\nBUCKET=\"$(date +%Y%m%d-%H 2>/dev/null)\"\n[ -z \"$BUCKET\" ] && BUCKET=\"unbucketed-$$\"\nMARKER=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.idle-escape-fired-${BUCKET}\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date '+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S')\"\n\n# Per-session 5-min cache (mirrors harness-health-self-scan.sh) to avoid re-firing\n# on every UserPromptSubmit within same session — perf budget on UserPromptSubmit\n# is <2s.\nCACHE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.idle-escape-cache-${SID}\"\nif [ -f \"$CACHE\" ]; then\n  CACHE_MTIME=$(stat -c %Y \"$CACHE\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$CACHE\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n  AGE=$(( $(date +%s) - CACHE_MTIME ))\n  if [ \"$AGE\" -lt 300 ]; then\n    echo \"[$TS] idle-escape-detector: CACHE-HIT age=${AGE}s session=$SID\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || true\n    exit 0\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Cleanup stale hour-bucket markers from prior buckets (L-S108-1 prevention).\nfor old_marker in \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"/.idle-escape-fired-*; do\n  [ -f \"$old_marker\" ] || continue\n  case \"$old_marker\" in\n    *\".idle-escape-fired-${BUCKET}\") ;;\n    *) rm -f \"$old_marker\" 2>/dev/null || true ;;\n  esac\ndone\n\n# Bail gracefully if current-execution.md missing.\nif [ ! -f \"$CE\" ]; then\n  touch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  exit 0\nfi\n\nset +o pipefail\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 1: pull last 5 session-block ## S<N> header lines (newest-first since\n# current-execution.md prepends new sessions at top).\n# ============================================================================\nHEADERS=$(grep -m 5 -E '^## S[0-9]+[a-z]?[[:space:]]+—[[:space:]]+Phase' \"$CE\" 2>/dev/null)\nHEADER_COUNT=$(echo \"$HEADERS\" | grep -c '^## S' 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n[[ \"$HEADER_COUNT\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || HEADER_COUNT=0\n\nif [ \"$HEADER_COUNT\" -lt 1 ]; then\n  touch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  echo \"session=$SID ts=$TS state=NO-HEADERS detail=cannot-parse-blocks\" > \"$CACHE\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 2: count consecutive ROUTINE-IDLE / META-CADENCE among newest blocks.\n# Stop counting at first non-idle header.\n# ============================================================================\nCONSECUTIVE=0\nLATEST_LABEL=\"\"\nLATEST_SID=\"\"\nwhile IFS= read -r line; do\n  [ -z \"$line\" ] && continue\n  if [ -z \"$LATEST_LABEL\" ]; then\n    LATEST_SID=$(echo \"$line\" | sed -E 's/^## (S[0-9]+[a-z]?).*/\\1/')\n    if echo \"$line\" | grep -qE '— ROUTINE-IDLE'; then LATEST_LABEL=\"ROUTINE-IDLE\"\n    elif echo \"$line\" | grep -qE '— META-CADENCE'; then LATEST_LABEL=\"META-CADENCE\"\n    else LATEST_LABEL=\"ACTIVE\"\n    fi\n  fi\n  if echo \"$line\" | grep -qE '— (ROUTINE-IDLE|META-CADENCE)'; then\n    CONSECUTIVE=$((CONSECUTIVE + 1))\n  else\n    break\n  fi\ndone <<< \"$HEADERS\"\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 3: scan session-plans/pending/ for queued plans (informational boost —\n# idle while plans are queued = stronger escape signal).\n# ============================================================================\nPENDING_PLANS=0\nif [ -d \"$PLANS_DIR\" ]; then\n  PENDING_PLANS=$(find \"$PLANS_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -name '*.md' -type f 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\n  [[ \"$PENDING_PLANS\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || PENDING_PLANS=0\nfi\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 4: severity classification.\n#   - GREEN: 0-2 consecutive idle\n#   - YELLOW: ≥3 consecutive idle, no pending plans\n#   - RED: ≥3 consecutive idle AND pending plans ≥1 (work queued + ignored)\n# Threshold ≥3 anchors to L-S171-1 / M-S171-1 doctrine.\n# ============================================================================\nSTATE=\"GREEN\"\nSEVERITY=\"LOW\"\nif [ \"$CONSECUTIVE\" -ge 3 ] && [ \"$PENDING_PLANS\" -ge 1 ]; then\n  STATE=\"RED\"\n  SEVERITY=\"HIGH\"\nelif [ \"$CONSECUTIVE\" -ge 3 ]; then\n  STATE=\"YELLOW\"\n  SEVERITY=\"MEDIUM\"\nfi\n\n# Cache the result regardless (so cache-hit fast-path holds for next 5 min).\n{\n  printf 'session=%s ts=%s state=%s severity=%s consecutive=%d latest_sid=%s latest_label=%s pending_plans=%d\\n' \\\n    \"$SID\" \"$TS\" \"$STATE\" \"$SEVERITY\" \"$CONSECUTIVE\" \"$LATEST_SID\" \"$LATEST_LABEL\" \"$PENDING_PLANS\"\n} > \"$CACHE\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\n# Always log a one-line summary for telemetry (passive observability).\necho \"[$TS] idle-escape-detector: state=$STATE consecutive=$CONSECUTIVE latest=$LATEST_SID(${LATEST_LABEL}) pending_plans=$PENDING_PLANS session=$SID\" \\\n  >> \"$HOOK_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\n# Idempotent fire: if marker already touched this hour-bucket, suppress system-reminder\n# emission BUT keep cache + log fresh (so observability doesn't go silent).\nif [ -f \"$MARKER\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Step 5: emit signal on RED/YELLOW.\n# ============================================================================\nif [ \"$STATE\" = \"GREEN\" ]; then\n  touch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Audit log entry capturing escape trigger (richer than .session-hooks.log).\n{\n  printf '[%s] %s state=%s consecutive=%d latest=%s(%s) pending_plans=%d session=%s event=%s\\n' \\\n    \"$TS\" \"ESCAPE-TRIGGER\" \"$STATE\" \"$CONSECUTIVE\" \"$LATEST_SID\" \"$LATEST_LABEL\" \"$PENDING_PLANS\" \"$SID\" \"$EVENT\"\n} >> \"$IDLE_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\nREMINDER_BODY=\"IDLE-ESCAPE-DETECTOR: state=$STATE (consecutive_idle=$CONSECUTIVE; latest=$LATEST_SID/$LATEST_LABEL; pending_plans=$PENDING_PLANS)\nPer L-S171-1 / M-S171-1 doctrine: ≥3 consecutive ROUTINE-IDLE / META-CADENCE blocks ⇒ phase-audit required BEFORE next idle.\nRequired action ONE OF:\n  (a) Dispatch phase-audit subagent (fresh-context Phase 2.5/3 status verify per verify_phase_before_next_phase doctrine);\n  (b) Open Q&A escalation bundle (human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/) if SCOPE/CHARTER ambiguity blocks substantive work;\n  (c) Pick + execute a queued session-plan from agent-workspace/session-plans/pending/ ($PENDING_PLANS available).\nForbidden: silent 4th ROUTINE-IDLE / META-CADENCE block. Charter Principle 8 + AP-5 binding.\"\n\n# UserPromptSubmit emission via Claude Code JSON contract (mirrors\n# harness-health-self-scan.sh § UserPromptSubmit emit; node may be unavailable\n# in stripped environments → fallback to stderr).\nif [ \"$EVENT\" = \"UserPromptSubmit\" ]; then\n  if command -v node >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    node -e \"process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({hookSpecificOutput:{hookEventName:'UserPromptSubmit',additionalContext:process.argv[1]}}))\" \"$REMINDER_BODY\" 2>/dev/null || \\\n      echo \"[idle-escape] $REMINDER_BODY\" >&2\n  else\n    echo \"[idle-escape] $REMINDER_BODY\" >&2\n  fi\nelif [ \"$EVENT\" = \"SessionStart\" ]; then\n  echo \"[idle-escape] $REMINDER_BODY\" >&2\nelse\n  echo \"[idle-escape] event=$EVENT $REMINDER_BODY\" >&2\nfi\n\ntouch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":73,"oldLines":8,"newStart":73,"newLines":8,"lines":[" # current-execution.md prepends new sessions at top)."," # ============================================================================"," HEADERS=$(grep -m 5 -E '^## S[0-9]+[a-z]?[[:space:]]+—[[:space:]]+Phase' \"$CE\" 2>/dev/null)","-HEADER_COUNT=$(echo \"$HEADERS\" | grep -c '^## S' 2>/dev/null || echo 0)","-[[ \"$HEADER_COUNT\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || HEADER_COUNT=0","+# L-S80-2 fix: clean integer 1/0 (downstream only checks -lt 1).","+if echo \"$HEADERS\" | grep -q '^## S' 2>/dev/null; then HEADER_COUNT=1; else HEADER_COUNT=0; fi"," "," if [ \"$HEADER_COUNT\" -lt 1 ]; then","   touch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"0c3793c0-009c-429d-acd5-c0b92d79edc3","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"22a3281c-5649-489a-a4fd-0f2f1ab6d011","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}
